def choose_start_mode():
    """
    Choose whether this run should:
      1. Build/place the cable and then optionally place symbols,
      2. Add V9 shields/twists to the cable already open in E3, or
      3. Run only the existing formatter.

    Symbols-only mode does NOT create/load/place the cable component.
    Formatter-only mode does not require an Excel workbook.
    """
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.title("Cable Placer")
    root.geometry("600x540")
    root.resizable(False, False)

    style = ttk.Style()
    try:
        style.theme_use("vista")
    except:
        pass

    style.configure("ModeTitle.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 18, "bold"))
    style.configure("ModeText.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 10))
    style.configure("ModeButton.TButton", font=("Segoe UI", 11, "bold"), padding=(18, 11))

    result = {"mode": None}

    def select_mode(mode):
        result["mode"] = mode
        root.destroy()

    frame = ttk.Frame(root, padding=25)
    frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text="Cable Placer",
        style="ModeTitle.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text="Choose what you want this run to do.",
        style="ModeText.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 22))

    ttk.Button(
        frame,
        text="Build / Place Cable",
        style="ModeButton.TButton",
        command=lambda: select_mode("FULL")
    ).pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text=(
            "Creates/loads and places the cable, formats the connector views, "
            "then pauses for manual connection lines before placing shields/twists "
            "when symbols are defined."
        ),
        justify="left",
        wraplength=535,
        style="ModeText.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 24))

    ttk.Button(
        frame,
        text="Add Symbols to Open Cable",
        style="ModeButton.TButton",
        command=lambda: select_mode("SYMBOLS_ONLY")
    ).pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text=(
            "Uses the cable already open in E3 and places"
            "shield/twist. It does not load or place the cable."
        ),
        justify="left",
        wraplength=535,
        style="ModeText.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    ttk.Button(
        frame,
        text="Simple Formatter Run",
        style="ModeButton.TButton",
        command=lambda: select_mode("FORMATTER_ONLY")
    ).pack(fill="x", pady=(24, 12))

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text=(
            "Runs only the existing Zuken Symbol Formatter on the cable already "
            "open in E3. No Excel file is requested and no cable or symbols "
            "are placed."
        ),
        justify="left",
        wraplength=535,
        style="ModeText.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    ttk.Button(
        frame,
        text="Cancel",
        command=lambda: select_mode(None)
    ).pack(pady=(22, 0))

    root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", lambda: select_mode(None))
    root.mainloop()

    return result["mode"]


# -------------------------------------------------------
# GUI SETUP
# -------------------------------------------------------
def create_gui():
    from tkinter import ttk
    import threading

    root = tk.Tk()
    root.title("Zuken Symbol Formatter")
    root.geometry("560x500")
    root.resizable(False, False)

    style = ttk.Style()
    try:
        style.theme_use("vista")
    except:
        pass

    style.configure("Title.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 18, "bold"))
    style.configure("Subtitle.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 10))
    style.configure("Step.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 10))
    style.configure("Status.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 10, "bold"))
    style.configure("Run.TButton", font=("Segoe UI", 11, "bold"), padding=(20, 10))
    style.configure("Secondary.TButton", font=("Segoe UI", 10), padding=(15, 8))

    main_frame = ttk.Frame(root, padding=25)
    main_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    ttk.Label(
        main_frame,
        text="Zuken Symbol Formatter",
        style="Title.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    ttk.Label(
        main_frame,
        text="Configure and format generated connector symbols.",
        style="Subtitle.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(2, 20))

    instruction_frame = ttk.LabelFrame(
        main_frame,
        text="Before Running",
        padding=15
    )
    instruction_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 20))

    instructions = (
        "1. Open Device Properties in Zuken.\n\n"
        "2. Enable:\n"
        "   Assembly  →  Additional Part ID\n\n"
        "3. Return here and click Run Formatter."
    )

    ttk.Label(
        instruction_frame,
        text=instructions,
        justify="left",
        style="Step.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    status_frame = ttk.LabelFrame(
        main_frame,
        text="Status",
        padding=12
    )
    status_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 20))

    status_label = ttk.Label(
        status_frame,
        text="Ready",
        style="Status.TLabel"
    )
    status_label.pack(anchor="w")

    # -------------------------------------------------------
    # FORMATTER FINISHED SUCCESSFULLY
    # -------------------------------------------------------
    def finished():
        root.config(cursor="")

        # Hide the formatter window immediately.
        root.withdraw()

        # Show completion popup.
        try:
            messagebox.showinfo(
                "Complete",
                "Formatting complete.",
                parent=root
            )
        finally:
            # Fully close formatter GUI after OK.
            root.destroy()

    # -------------------------------------------------------
    # FORMATTER ERROR
    # -------------------------------------------------------
    def failed(error, details):
        status_label.config(text="Formatting failed.")
        run_button.config(state="normal")
        run_again_button.config(state="normal")
        root.config(cursor="")

        show_error_dialog(
            error,
            parent=root,
            details=details
        )

    # -------------------------------------------------------
    # BACKGROUND FORMATTER WORKER
    # -------------------------------------------------------

    def worker():
        error = None
        details = None
        com_initialized = False

        try:
            pythoncom.CoInitialize()
            com_initialized = True

            run_ct_code()

        except Exception as e:
            error = e
            details = traceback.format_exc()
            print(details)

        finally:
            if com_initialized:
                pythoncom.CoUninitialize()

        if error is None:
            root.after(0, finished)
        else:
            root.after(
                0,
                lambda err=error, trace=details: failed(err, trace)
            )
    # -------------------------------------------------------
    # START FORMATTER
    # -------------------------------------------------------
    def run_formatter():
        status_label.config(text="Running...")
        run_button.config(state="disabled")
        run_again_button.config(state="disabled")
        root.config(cursor="watch")

        threading.Thread(
            target=worker,
            daemon=True
        ).start()

    # -------------------------------------------------------
    # CLOSE
    # -------------------------------------------------------
    def close_app():
        root.destroy()

    # -------------------------------------------------------
    # BUTTONS
    # -------------------------------------------------------
    button_frame = ttk.Frame(main_frame)
    button_frame.pack(fill="x")

    run_button = ttk.Button(
        button_frame,
        text="Run Formatter",
        style="Run.TButton",
        command=run_formatter
    )
    run_button.pack(fill="x")

    secondary_frame = ttk.Frame(main_frame)
    secondary_frame.pack(pady=(12, 0))

    run_again_button = ttk.Button(
        secondary_frame,
        text="Run Again",
        style="Secondary.TButton",
        command=run_formatter
    )
    run_again_button.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))

    ttk.Button(
        secondary_frame,
        text="Close",
        style="Secondary.TButton",
        command=close_app
    ).pack(side="left")

    ttk.Label(
        main_frame,
        text="E3 Symbol Automation",
        font=("Segoe UI", 8)
    ).pack(side="bottom", anchor="e", pady=(15, 0))

    root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", close_app)

    root.mainloop()

# -------------------------------------------------------
# CONNECTION-LINE CHECKPOINT
#
# This window is only shown when the selected workbook contains
# at least one shield or twist definition.
# -------------------------------------------------------
def wait_for_connection_lines():
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.title("Connect Cable Lines")
    root.geometry("560x360")
    root.resizable(False, False)

    style = ttk.Style()
    try:
        style.theme_use("vista")
    except:
        pass

    style.configure("ConnectTitle.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 18, "bold"))
    style.configure("ConnectStep.TLabel", font=("Segoe UI", 10))
    style.configure(
        "ConnectButton.TButton",
        font=("Segoe UI", 11, "bold"),
        padding=(20, 10)
    )

    result = {"continue": False}

    def continue_to_symbols():
        result["continue"] = True
        root.destroy()

    def cancel():
        result["continue"] = False
        root.destroy()

    frame = ttk.Frame(root, padding=25)
    frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text="Place Symbols",
        style="ConnectTitle.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w")

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text="CONNECT ALL PINS AND CONNECTORS BEFORE PLACING SYMBOLS!",
        style="ConnectStep.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 18))

    instructions = (
        "Click to Place Shields / Twists."
    )

    ttk.Label(
        frame,
        text=instructions,
        justify="left",
        style="ConnectStep.TLabel"
    ).pack(anchor="w", fill="x")

    button_frame = ttk.Frame(frame)
    button_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(25, 0))

    ttk.Button(
        button_frame,
        text="Place Shields / Twists",
        style="ConnectButton.TButton",
        command=continue_to_symbols
    ).pack(fill="x")

    ttk.Button(
        button_frame,
        text="Cancel",
        command=cancel
    ).pack(pady=(10, 0))

    root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", cancel)
    root.mainloop()

    return result["continue"]


def run_ct_code():
    try:
        app = win32.gencache.EnsureDispatch('CT.Application')
        prj = app.CreateJobObject()
        text = prj.CreateTextObject()
        dev = prj.CreateDeviceObject()
        sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()

        # =====================================================
        # SCHEMATIC-VIEW ADDITIONAL PART ID
        #
        # Keep exactly ONE Additional Part ID across ALL split
        # schematic pin views.
        #
        # The text is attached to the overall top-left main pin
        # symbol, but moved to the horizontal midpoint between the
        # LEFT and RIGHT main pin columns and slightly above the
        # highest main pin.
        #
        # This prevents one Additional Part ID from appearing on
        # both the left and right sides.
        # =====================================================
        all_schematic_symbol_locations = {}

        for scan_dev_id in new_dev_ids:
            dev.SetId(scan_dev_id)

            scan_symbol_info = dev.GetSymbolIds(None)
            scan_symbol_ids = [
                x for x in scan_symbol_info[1]
                if x is not None
            ]

            for scan_symbol_id in scan_symbol_ids:
                sym.SetId(scan_symbol_id)

                xloc = [0.0]
                yloc = [0.0]
                grid = [""]
                column = [""]
                row = [""]

                try:
                    location = sym.GetSchemaLocation(
                        xloc,
                        yloc,
                        grid,
                        column,
                        row
                    )

                    if isinstance(location, tuple) and len(location) >= 3:
                        all_schematic_symbol_locations[scan_symbol_id] = (
                            float(location[1]),
                            float(location[2])
                        )
                except Exception:
                    pass

        schematic_additional_part_symbol_id = None
        schematic_additional_part_x = None
        schematic_additional_part_y = None

        # PINN_LOCS contains the actual main pin-column X locations
        # generated by the cable placer.  Spare pins use SPARE_X and
        # therefore are not included here.
        main_pin_x_values = sorted(set(
            round(float(location[1]), 6)
            for location in PINN_LOCS
        ))

        if all_schematic_symbol_locations and main_pin_x_values:
            left_main_x = min(main_pin_x_values)
            right_main_x = max(main_pin_x_values)

            # Center the single label between the two cable sides.
            schematic_additional_part_x = (
                left_main_x + right_main_x
            ) / 2.0

            main_symbol_ids = [
                symbol_id
                for symbol_id, location
                in all_schematic_symbol_locations.items()
                if (
                    abs(location[0] - left_main_x) <= 0.001
                    or abs(location[0] - right_main_x) <= 0.001
                )
            ]

            if main_symbol_ids:
                top_main_y = max(
                    all_schematic_symbol_locations[symbol_id][1]
                    for symbol_id in main_symbol_ids
                )

                # Keep the text just above the top pin.
                schematic_additional_part_y = top_main_y +0.15

                # Use the top-most symbol on the LEFT side as the single
                # owner of the type-1000 text object.
                left_symbol_ids = [
                    symbol_id
                    for symbol_id in main_symbol_ids
                    if abs(
                        all_schematic_symbol_locations[symbol_id][0]
                        - left_main_x
                    ) <= 0.001
                ]

                if left_symbol_ids:
                    schematic_additional_part_symbol_id = max(
                        left_symbol_ids,
                        key=lambda symbol_id:
                            all_schematic_symbol_locations[symbol_id][1]
                    )
                else:
                    schematic_additional_part_symbol_id = max(
                        main_symbol_ids,
                        key=lambda symbol_id:
                            all_schematic_symbol_locations[symbol_id][1]
                    )

                print(
                    "SINGLE SCHEMATIC ADDITIONAL PART ID OWNER:",
                    schematic_additional_part_symbol_id
                )
                print(
                    "SCHEMATIC ADDITIONAL PART ID CENTER:",
                    schematic_additional_part_x,
                    schematic_additional_part_y
                )

        # =====================================================
        # LOOP THROUGH EACH NEW DEVICE VIEW
        # =====================================================

        for new_dev_id in new_dev_ids:
            connector = NEW_DEV_CONNECTORS.get(new_dev_id)

            print("\n======================================")
            print("NEW VIEW ID:", new_dev_id)
            print("CONNECTOR:", connector)
            print("======================================")

            if connector is None:
                print("WARNING: no connector mapping for view", new_dev_id)
                continue

            dev.SetId(new_dev_id)
            symbol_info = dev.GetSymbolIds(None)
            symbol_ids = [x for x in symbol_info[1] if x is not None]
            print("NEW VIEW SYMBOL IDS:", symbol_ids)

            for symbol_id in symbol_ids:
                sym.SetId(symbol_id)
                print("new view symbol:",  symbol_id, sym.GetName())
                texinfo = sym.GetTextIds(None)
                text_ids = [x for x in texinfo[1] if x is not None]

                for t_id in text_ids:
                    text.SetId(t_id)
                    text_type = text.GetType()

                    print(
                        "PIN SYMBOL:",
                        sym.GetName(),
                        "| text:",
                        text.GetText(),
                        "| type:",
                        text_type
                    )

                    if text_type == 5:
                        text.SetVisibility(0)

                    elif text_type == 1000:
                        if (
                            symbol_id
                            == schematic_additional_part_symbol_id
                            and schematic_additional_part_x is not None
                            and schematic_additional_part_y is not None
                        ):
                            app.PutMessage(
                                "Configuring single centered schematic "
                                "Additional Part ID"
                            )

                            text.SetVisibility(1)
                            text.SetStyle(1)
                            text.SetHeight(0.2)
                            text.SetColour(0)
                            text.SetMode(1)
                            text.SetAlignment(2)
                            text.SetSchemaLocation(
                                schematic_additional_part_x,
                                schematic_additional_part_y
                            )
                        else:
                            # Every other split-view Additional Part ID,
                            # including the previous top-right one, is hidden.
                            text.SetVisibility(0)

                    if text_type == 12:
                        text.SetStyle(1)

            connector_id = CONNECTORS.get(connector)
            print("CONNECTOR:",  connector, "ID:",  connector_id)

            if connector_id is None:
                print("WARNING: connector ID not found for", connector)
                continue

            dev.SetId(connector_id)
            print("Formatting bulk connector:", dev.GetName())


            assydevsymbolinfo = dev.GetSymbolIds(None)
            assydevsymbolids = [x for x in assydevsymbolinfo[1] if x is not None]
            print("BULK SYMBOL IDS:", assydevsymbolids)

            for assydevsymbol_id in assydevsymbolids:
                sym.SetId(assydevsymbol_id)
                print("BULK SYMBOL:", assydevsymbol_id, "| name:", sym.GetName())
                assydevsymboltextinfo = sym.GetTextIds(None)
                assydevsymboltextids = [x for x in assydevsymboltextinfo[1] if x is not None]

                for assydevsymboltext_id in assydevsymboltextids:
                    text.SetId(assydevsymboltext_id)
                    print("name:", sym.GetName(), "| text:", text.GetText(), "| type:", text.GetType())
                    if text.GetType() == 5:
                        text.SetVisibility(0)
                    if text.GetType() == 12:
                        text.SetStyle(1)
                    if (text.GetType() == 1000 and sym.GetName() == "J1"):
                        app.PutMessage("Configuring text")
                        text.SetVisibility(1)
                        text.SetStyle(1)
                        text.SetHeight(0.2)
                        text.SetColour(0)
                        text.SetMode(1)
                        text.SetAlignment(2)
                        # A formatter-only pass must NEVER shift an already
                        # positioned top-level (bulk J1) Additional Part ID.
                        # The previous +1.5 / +0.15 adjustment was relative
                        # to its current location, so each formatter run
                        # moved the label farther away.  Preserve its actual
                        # E3 location in FORMATTER_ONLY; keep the original
                        # first-placement behavior in the full cable workflow.
                        if START_MODE != "FORMATTER_ONLY":
                            xloc = [0.0]
                            yloc = [0.0]
                            grid = [""]
                            column = [""]
                            row = [""]
                            result = text.GetSchemaLocation(xloc, yloc, grid, column, row)
                            new_x = result[1] + 1.5
                            new_y = result[2] + 0.15
                            text.SetSchemaLocation(new_x, new_y)
                        else:
                            print(
                                "FORMATTER ONLY: keeping top-level Additional "
                                "Part ID at its existing schematic location"
                            )

                    elif text.GetType() == 1000:
                        text.SetVisibility(0)

        del text
        del sym
        del dev
        del prj
        del app
        print("E3 formatter completed successfully.")

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"E3 formattor error: {e}")
        raise


def ask_save_path(default_full_path=""):
    # Optional E3 Mega Tools workbook selection. Fall back to this tool's own picker.
    import os as _mega_os
    _mega_excel = _mega_os.environ.get("E3_MEGA_CABLE_WORKBOOK", "").strip()
    if _mega_excel:
        if not _mega_os.path.isfile(_mega_excel):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Selected cable workbook no longer exists: {_mega_excel}")
        return _mega_os.path.abspath(_mega_excel)

    import os
    import tkinter as tk
    from tkinter import filedialog

    default_folder = os.path.dirname(default_full_path)

    # Create Tk root
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.withdraw()
    root.attributes("-topmost", True)
    root.update()

    try:
        # Let user PICK an existing Excel file
        selected_path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
            initialdir=default_folder,
            filetypes=[("Excel Files", "*.xlsx"), ("All Files", "*.*")]
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    # Cancel or X
    if not selected_path:
        root.destroy()
        return None
    root.destroy()
    # Return ONLY the file name (not full path)
    return os.path.abspath(selected_path)

def generate_ordered_pin_ids(dict_to_order, dest):
    return {str(pin): dict_to_order[str(pin)] for pin in dest}


def get_connector_pin_ids(dev, device_id, connector_name="", prefer_all=False):
    """
    Return the actual project pin IDs for a connector/device view.

    Base connector devices are queried with GetPinIds first because those are
    the physical connector pins.  Created schematic views are queried with
    GetAllPinIds first because their view pins are what PlacePins needs.

    The alternate method is used only as a fallback.  This keeps the old
    behavior available while fixing connectors for which GetAllPinIds returns
    an empty list on the base device.
    """
    dev.SetId(device_id)

    if prefer_all:
        method_names = ("GetAllPinIds", "GetPinIds")
    else:
        method_names = ("GetPinIds", "GetAllPinIds")

    for method_name in method_names:
        method = getattr(dev, method_name, None)
        if method is None:
            continue

        # E3 COM methods can differ between generated wrappers, so support
        # both the explicit output-array form and the no-argument form.
        for use_none in (True, False):
            try:
                result = method(None) if use_none else method()
            except Exception as error:
                print(
                    "PIN ENUMERATION FAILED:",
                    connector_name,
                    "DEVICE:", device_id,
                    "METHOD:", method_name,
                    "ERROR:", repr(error)
                )
                continue

            pin_ids = extract_e3_ids(result)

            print(
                "PIN ENUMERATION:",
                connector_name,
                "DEVICE:", device_id,
                "METHOD:", method_name,
                "RAW:", repr(result),
                "PIN IDS:", pin_ids
            )

            if pin_ids:
                return pin_ids

    return []


def connector_designation_from_device_name(value):
    """
    Convert an E3 connector name/reference to the workbook layout designation.

    Current cable-maker output uses W1.<designation>, e.g. W1.9B.  The older
    W1.9B / P9B form is still accepted for numeric designations.
    """
    return normalize_connector_token(value)


def generate_placement_locations(Pins, layout, pin_spacing=0.20):
    TOP_OF_PAGE = 32.9
    BOTTOM_OF_PAGE = 0.0
    LEFT_OF_PAGE = 0.0
    RIGHT_OF_PAGE = 42.9

    PIN_OFFSET_FROM_CONN = 3.0

    X_START = 1.0
    Y_START = 32.5
    main_conn_locs = [] # conn, x, y, flip

    # Center the shorter connector column vertically against the longer one.
    # Example:
    #   LEFT  = [P1, P3, P4]
    #   RIGHT = [P2]
    # gives P2 the same Y as P3.
    active_rows = [
        [conn for conn in row if conn is not None]
        for row in layout
    ]

    max_connector_count = max(
        (len(row) for row in active_rows),
        default=0
    )

    for i, row in enumerate(active_rows):
        vertical_offset = (max_connector_count - len(row)) / 2.0

        for j, conn in enumerate(row):
            flip = i > 0
            y_index = j + vertical_offset

            main_conn_locs.append([
                conn,
                X_START + (i * 3.0),
                Y_START - (y_index * 1.0),
                flip
            ])

    y_pin_start = 32.9
    for r in main_conn_locs:
        if y_pin_start > r[2]:
            y_pin_start = r[2]
    y_pin_start -= PIN_OFFSET_FROM_CONN

    print(Pins)
    print(layout)

    pin_locs = [] # conn, xstart, ystart, flip

    for dictionary in Pins:
        # Actual connector designation from the E3 device name.
        # Examples: W1.1 -> "1", W1.9B -> "9B".
        conn_key = list(dictionary.keys())[0]
        conn = connector_designation_from_device_name(conn_key)

        # number of pins = all keys except the "W1.<designation>"
        count = len(dictionary) - 1

        top = True
        flip = 0
        xstart = 0
        ystart = 0

        # find this connector's placement data
        for r in main_conn_locs:
            if str(r[0]) == conn:
                xstart = r[1]
                ystart = r[2]
                flip = r[3]

        # determine if connector is below another connector
        for r in main_conn_locs:
            if r[3] == flip:       # same flip side (top or bottom)
                if ystart < r[2]:  # this connector is below another
                    top = False

        if top:
            # normal top placement
            ystart = y_pin_start
        else:
            # Below another connector, use the same D15-selected vertical
            # wire spacing as the actual placed schematic pin views.

            pins_above = 0
            conns_above = 0

            # iterate over all pin dictionaries ABOVE our current connector
            for r in Pins:
                r_conn_key = list(r.keys())[0]
                r_conn = connector_designation_from_device_name(r_conn_key)

                if r_conn == conn:
                    break  # stop when reaching our connector

                # check if the connector is above AND same flip
                for c in main_conn_locs:
                    if str(c[0]) == r_conn and c[3] == flip:
                        if c[2] > ystart:
                            pins_above += (len(r) - 1)
                            conns_above += 1

            ystart = y_pin_start - (pins_above * pin_spacing) - (conns_above * 0.5)

        pin_locs.append([conn, xstart, ystart, flip])

    return main_conn_locs, pin_locs, y_pin_start + 2

def generate_aligned_pin_y(pinouts, y_start, pin_spacing=0.20, connector_gap=0.5):
    pin_y = {}

    current_y = y_start

    previous_c1 = None
    previous_c2 = None

    for row_index, (c1, p1, c2, p2) in enumerate(pinouts):
        if c1 is None or c2 is None:
            continue

        # Add a gap whenever we move into a new connector group
        # on EITHER side of the cable.
        if previous_c1 is not None:
            connector_changed = (
                c1 != previous_c1
                or c2 != previous_c2
            )

            if connector_changed:
                current_y -= connector_gap

        # Both ends of this wire get EXACTLY the same Y.
        pin_y[(str(c1), str(p1))] = current_y
        pin_y[(str(c2), str(p2))] = current_y

        current_y -= pin_spacing

        previous_c1 = c1
        previous_c2 = c2

    return pin_y

def get_spare_pins(pins, pinouts):
    spares = {}

    # Build quick connector -> pin dictionary lookup
    pin_lookup = {}

    for pindict in pins:
        connector = list(pindict.keys())[0]
        pin_lookup[connector] = pindict

    for c1, p1, c2, p2 in pinouts:

        # ==========================================
        # SPARE ON SIDE 1
        # ==========================================
        if c1 is not None and p1 is not None and c2 is None:
            connector = f"W1.{c1}"

            if connector not in spares:
                spares[connector] = {}

            if connector in pin_lookup:
                pin_id = pin_lookup[connector].get(str(p1))

                if pin_id is not None:
                    spares[connector][str(p1)] = pin_id

        # ==========================================
        # SPARE ON SIDE 2
        # ==========================================
        elif c2 is not None and p2 is not None and c1 is None:
            connector = f"W1.{c2}"

            if connector not in spares:
                spares[connector] = {}

            if connector in pin_lookup:
                pin_id = pin_lookup[connector].get(str(p2))

                if pin_id is not None:
                    spares[connector][str(p2)] = pin_id

    return spares

# This file is intentionally based directly on the script the user
# confirmed resized/placed Shield_Demo correctly.
#
# Do NOT change these working E3 behaviors:
#   - outer shields are attempted FIRST
#   - inner shields are attempted before twists
#   - each shield attempt gets a fresh CreateSymbolObject()
#   - Shield_Demo uses Load -> Place -> immediate SetDisplayLength
#   - SetId/GetDisplayLength happen only AFTER that immediate stretch attempt
#
# Outer shields are now split at every connector change: P1, P3 and P4
# each receive a separate physical shield, even when Excel marks continue.
# ======================================================================

#====================================================================================================================================
# FILE PICKER

def ask_excel_path():
    root = tk.Tk()
    root.withdraw()
    root.attributes("-topmost", True)
    root.update()
    selected_path = filedialog.askopenfilename(title="Select Cable Symbol Placement Excel File", filetypes=[("Excel Files", "*.xlsx"), ("Excel Macro Files", "*.xlsm"), ("All Files", "*.*")])
    root.destroy()

    if not selected_path:
        return None

    return os.path.abspath(selected_path)


#====================================================================================================================================
# VALUE HELPERS
#====================================================================================================================================
def clean_excel_value(value):
    if value is None:
        return ""

    if isinstance(value, int):
        return str(value)

    if isinstance(value, float):
        if value.is_integer():
            return str(int(value))

        return str(value).strip()

    return str(value).strip()


def normalize_pin_name(value):
    return clean_excel_value(value).upper()


def normalize_connector_token(value):
    """
    Normalize an Excel/E3 connector reference to the layout designation only.

    Examples:
        3       -> "3"
        P3      -> "3"
        W1.3    -> "3"
        W1.P3   -> "3"
        9B      -> "9B"
        P9B     -> "9B"
        W1.9B   -> "9B"

    A leading P is removed only for the old P<number> form.  Names such as
    PMOD are left alone.
    """
    text = clean_excel_value(value).upper().replace(" ", "")

    if text == "":
        return ""

    root_prefix = CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME.upper() + "."

    if text.startswith(root_prefix):
        text = text[len(root_prefix):]

    if "#" in text:
        text = text.split("#", 1)[0]

    match = re.fullmatch(r"P?0*(\d+)([A-Z]*)", text)

    if match:
        number = int(match.group(1))
        suffix = match.group(2)
        return f"{number}{suffix}"

    return text


def connector_base_name(connector_token):
    connector = normalize_connector_token(connector_token)

    if connector == "":
        return ""

    # Cable-maker assembly members are named %.<designation>, so E3 exposes
    # them as W1.<designation> (for example W1.3 and W1.9B), NOT W1.P3.
    return f"{CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME}.{connector}"


def cell_has_bar(value):
    return "|" in clean_excel_value(value)


#====================================================================================================================================
# YES / NO SETTING
#====================================================================================================================================
def parse_yes_no(value, setting_name):
    text = clean_excel_value(value).upper()

    if text == "":
        print("WARNING:", setting_name, "IS BLANK - DEFAULTING TO YES")
        return True

    if text in ["YES", "Y", "TRUE", "1"]:
        return True

    if text in ["NO", "N", "FALSE", "0"]:
        return False

    raise Exception(f"{setting_name} must contain YES or NO. Current value: {value}")


#====================================================================================================================================
# READ CABLE PIN SPACING (D15)
#====================================================================================================================================
def read_pin_spacing_setting(ws):
    selected = clean_excel_value(ws[PIN_SPACING_MODE_CELL].value)
    normalized = " ".join(selected.upper().split())

    if not normalized:
        print(
            "D15 is blank; defaulting to Default Zuken Spacing (0.20)."
        )
        normalized = "DEFAULT ZUKEN SPACING"

    if normalized == "DEFAULT ZUKEN SPACING":
        return "Default Zuken Spacing", DEFAULT_ZUKEN_PIN_SPACING

    if normalized == "INCREASED SPACING":
        return "Increased Spacing", INCREASED_PIN_SPACING

    raise ValueError(
        f"Cell {PIN_SPACING_MODE_CELL} must be 'Default Zuken Spacing' "
        f"or 'Increased Spacing'. Found: {selected!r}"
    )


#====================================================================================================================================
# READ TWIST OFFSET SETTINGS
#
# J2:K2 merged -> J2
# R2:S2 merged -> R2
#====================================================================================================================================
def read_twist_offset_settings(ws):
    left_enabled = parse_yes_no(ws[LEFT_TWIST_OFFSET_CELL].value, "LEFT TWIST OFFSET J2:K2")
    right_enabled = parse_yes_no(ws[RIGHT_TWIST_OFFSET_CELL].value, "RIGHT TWIST OFFSET R2:S2")

    settings = {
        "LEFT": left_enabled,
        "RIGHT": right_enabled
    }

    return settings


#====================================================================================================================================
# FIND LAST DATA ROW
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_last_data_row(ws):
    columns = [LEFT_OUTER_SHIELD_COL, LEFT_INNER_SHIELD_COL, LEFT_TWIST_A_COL, LEFT_TWIST_B_COL, LEFT_CONNECTOR_COL, PIN_1_COL, PIN_2_COL, RIGHT_CONNECTOR_COL, RIGHT_TWIST_A_COL, RIGHT_TWIST_B_COL, RIGHT_INNER_SHIELD_COL, RIGHT_OUTER_SHIELD_COL]
    scan_end = min(max(ws.max_row, DATA_FIRST_ROW), DATA_FIRST_ROW + MAX_SCAN_ROWS)
    last_row = DATA_FIRST_ROW - 1

    for row in range(DATA_FIRST_ROW, scan_end + 1):
        for col in columns:
            value = clean_excel_value(ws[f"{col}{row}"].value)

            if value != "":
                last_row = row
                break

    return last_row


#====================================================================================================================================
# READ LEFT OR RIGHT SIDE
#====================================================================================================================================
def read_side_rows(ws, side, last_row):
    if side == "LEFT":
        connector_col = LEFT_CONNECTOR_COL
        pin_col = PIN_1_COL
        twist_a_col = LEFT_TWIST_A_COL
        twist_b_col = LEFT_TWIST_B_COL
        inner_col = LEFT_INNER_SHIELD_COL
        outer_col = LEFT_OUTER_SHIELD_COL
    else:
        connector_col = RIGHT_CONNECTOR_COL
        pin_col = PIN_2_COL
        twist_a_col = RIGHT_TWIST_A_COL
        twist_b_col = RIGHT_TWIST_B_COL
        inner_col = RIGHT_INNER_SHIELD_COL
        outer_col = RIGHT_OUTER_SHIELD_COL

    rows = []
    current_connector = ""

    for excel_row in range(DATA_FIRST_ROW, last_row + 1):
        connector_value = clean_excel_value(ws[f"{connector_col}{excel_row}"].value)
        pin_name = normalize_pin_name(ws[f"{pin_col}{excel_row}"].value)

        if connector_value != "":
            current_connector = normalize_connector_token(connector_value)

        if pin_name == "":
            continue

        row_info = {
            "side": side,
            "excel_row": excel_row,
            "connector": current_connector,
            "pin": pin_name,
            "twist_a": cell_has_bar(ws[f"{twist_a_col}{excel_row}"].value),
            "twist_b": cell_has_bar(ws[f"{twist_b_col}{excel_row}"].value),
            "inner_shield": cell_has_bar(ws[f"{inner_col}{excel_row}"].value),
            "outer_shield": cell_has_bar(ws[f"{outer_col}{excel_row}"].value)
        }

        rows.append(row_info)

    return rows


#====================================================================================================================================
# SPLIT INTO CONNECTOR SECTIONS
#====================================================================================================================================
def split_into_sections(rows):
    sections = []
    current = []

    for row in rows:
        if not current:
            current = [row]
            continue

        previous = current[-1]
        connector_changed = row["connector"] != previous["connector"]
        row_gap = row["excel_row"] != previous["excel_row"] + 1

        if connector_changed or row_gap:
            sections.append(current)
            current = [row]
        else:
            current.append(row)

    if current:
        sections.append(current)

    return sections


#====================================================================================================================================
# CONTIGUOUS MARKED RANGES
#====================================================================================================================================
def true_ranges(rows, key):
    ranges = []
    start = None

    for index, row in enumerate(rows):
        marked = bool(row[key])

        if marked and start is None:
            start = index

        if not marked and start is not None:
            ranges.append((start, index - 1))
            start = None

    if start is not None:
        ranges.append((start, len(rows) - 1))

    return ranges


def range_pins(rows, start, end):
    pins = []

    for index in range(start, end + 1):
        pins.append(rows[index]["pin"])

    return pins


def range_excel_rows(rows, start, end):
    excel_rows = []

    for index in range(start, end + 1):
        excel_rows.append(rows[index]["excel_row"])

    return excel_rows



#====================================================================================================================================
# BUILD TWIST / INNER-SHIELD JOBS FOR ONE CONNECTOR SECTION
#
# INNER SHIELD RULE:
#   Inner SHLD does NOT create a standalone shield.
#   It only places Shield_Demo around a twist run when every wire in
#   that twist run is marked in the inner-SHLD column.
#
# OUTER SHIELDS ARE BUILT SEPARATELY AND SPLIT AT CONNECTOR CHANGES,
# EVEN WHEN OUTER-SHIELD MARKS CONTINUE (EXAMPLE: P1 -> P3 -> P4).
#====================================================================================================================================
def build_section_jobs(rows):
    jobs = []
    warnings = []

    if not rows:
        return jobs, warnings

    side = rows[0]["side"]
    connector = rows[0]["connector"]

    if connector == "":
        warnings.append(f"{side}: rows {rows[0]['excel_row']}-{rows[-1]['excel_row']} have pins but no connector")
        return jobs, warnings

    conflicts = []

    for row in rows:
        if row["twist_a"] and row["twist_b"]:
            conflicts.append(row["excel_row"])

    if conflicts:
        message = f"{side} {connector}: same wire marked in both twist columns on Excel rows {conflicts}"

        if STRICT_TWIST_VALIDATION:
            raise Exception(message)

        warnings.append(message)

    twist_jobs = []

    for key, twist_column in [("twist_a", "A"), ("twist_b", "B")]:
        ranges = true_ranges(rows, key)

        for start, end in ranges:
            pins = range_pins(rows, start, end)
            excel_rows = range_excel_rows(rows, start, end)

            if len(pins) < 2:
                warnings.append(
                    f"{side} {connector}: twist {twist_column} only contains one wire "
                    f"on Excel row {excel_rows[0]}"
                )
                continue

            job = {
                "type": "twist",
                "side": side,
                "connector": connector,
                "twist_column": twist_column,
                "start": start,
                "end": end,
                "pins": pins,
                "excel_rows": excel_rows,
                "symbol_name": TWIST_SYMBOL_NAME
            }

            twist_jobs.append(job)
            jobs.append(job)

    # Inner SHLD only wraps an existing twist. It never makes its own span.
    for twist_job in twist_jobs:
        indexes = list(range(twist_job["start"], twist_job["end"] + 1))
        inner_marks = [bool(rows[index]["inner_shield"]) for index in indexes]

        if all(inner_marks):
            jobs.append({
                "type": "shield_inner",
                "side": side,
                "connector": connector,
                "twist_column": twist_job["twist_column"],
                "pins": list(twist_job["pins"]),
                "excel_rows": list(twist_job["excel_rows"]),
                "symbol_name": SHIELD_SYMBOL_NAME
            })
        elif any(inner_marks):
            warnings.append(
                f"{side} {connector}: inner SHLD partially covers twist "
                f"{twist_job['twist_column']} on Excel rows {twist_job['excel_rows']}; "
                f"inner shield ignored because the full twist must be marked"
            )

    return jobs, warnings


#====================================================================================================================================
# BUILD ONE OUTER-SHIELD JOB PER CONTIGUOUS RUN ON ONE CONNECTOR
#
# A connector change ends the current shield, even when both rows are
# marked in Excel.  Blank Excel row gaps and unmarked rows also end it.
# Each new connector gets its own Shield_Demo and its own calculated span.
#====================================================================================================================================
def build_outer_shield_jobs(rows):
    jobs = []

    if not rows:
        return jobs

    side = rows[0]["side"]
    start = None

    def append_job(start_index, end_index):
        covered_rows = rows[start_index:end_index + 1]

        connector = covered_rows[0]["connector"]
        if any(row["connector"] != connector for row in covered_rows):
            raise ValueError("Outer shield job must contain exactly one connector")

        jobs.append({
            "type": "shield_outer",
            "side": side,
            "connector": connector,
            "connectors": [connector],
            "rows": covered_rows,
            "pins": [row["pin"] for row in covered_rows],
            "excel_rows": [row["excel_row"] for row in covered_rows],
            "twist_column": None,
            "symbol_name": SHIELD_SYMBOL_NAME
        })

    for index, row in enumerate(rows):
        marked = bool(row["outer_shield"])

        if start is not None:
            previous = rows[index - 1]
            row_gap = row["excel_row"] != previous["excel_row"] + 1
            connector_changed = row["connector"] != previous["connector"]

            if row_gap or connector_changed or not marked:
                append_job(start, index - 1)
                start = None

        if marked and start is None:
            start = index

    if start is not None:
        append_job(start, len(rows) - 1)

    return jobs


#====================================================================================================================================
# E3 ID EXTRACTOR
#====================================================================================================================================
def extract_e3_ids(result):
    if result is None:
        return []

    if isinstance(result, int):
        if result > 0:
            return [result]

        return []

    if isinstance(result, list):
        output = []

        for value in result:
            if value is not None:
                try:
                    if int(value) > 0:
                        output.append(int(value))
                except Exception:
                    pass

        return output

    if not isinstance(result, tuple):
        return []

    if len(result) == 0:
        return []

    if len(result) >= 2:
        values = result[1]

        if values is None:
            return []

        if isinstance(values, int):
            if values > 0:
                return [values]

            return []

        try:
            return [int(value) for value in values if value is not None and int(value) > 0]
        except Exception:
            pass

    output = []

    for value in result:
        try:
            if int(value) > 0:
                output.append(int(value))
        except Exception:
            pass

    return output


#====================================================================================================================================
# GET ALL BASE DEVICES
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_all_devices(prj, dev):
    info = prj.GetAllDeviceIds(None)
    ids = extract_e3_ids(info)
    records = []

    for device_id in ids:
        dev.SetId(device_id)

        try:
            name = clean_excel_value(dev.GetName())
        except Exception:
            continue

        records.append({"device_id": device_id, "name": name})

    return records


#====================================================================================================================================
# READ PIN SCHEMATIC LOCATION
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_pin_location(pin, pin_id):
    pin.SetId(pin_id)

    xloc = [0.0]
    yloc = [0.0]
    grid = [""]
    column = [""]
    row = [""]

    try:
        result = pin.GetSchemaLocation(xloc, yloc, grid, column, row)
    except Exception:
        return None

    if not isinstance(result, tuple):
        return None

    if len(result) < 3:
        return None

    try:
        sheet_id = int(result[0])
        x = float(result[1])
        y = float(result[2])
    except Exception:
        return None

    if sheet_id <= 0:
        return None

    return {"sheet_id": sheet_id, "x": x, "y": y}


#====================================================================================================================================
# READ SYMBOL SCHEMATIC LOCATION
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_symbol_location(sym, symbol_id):
    sym.SetId(symbol_id)

    xloc = [0.0]
    yloc = [0.0]
    grid = [""]
    column = [""]
    row = [""]

    try:
        result = sym.GetSchemaLocation(xloc, yloc, grid, column, row)
    except Exception:
        return None

    if not isinstance(result, tuple):
        return None

    if len(result) < 3:
        return None

    try:
        sheet_id = int(result[0])
        x = float(result[1])
        y = float(result[2])
    except Exception:
        return None

    if sheet_id <= 0:
        return None

    return {"sheet_id": sheet_id, "x": x, "y": y}


#====================================================================================================================================
# LOAD DEVICE PIN MAP
#====================================================================================================================================
def load_device_pin_map(dev, pin, device_id):
    # Use the SAME view-pin enumeration fallback as the working placer.
    # Some E3 views expose pins through GetPinIds rather than GetAllPinIds.
    pin_ids = get_connector_pin_ids(
        dev, device_id, connector_name=f"overlay candidate {device_id}",
        prefer_all=True
    )
    pin_map = {}

    for pin_id in pin_ids:
        pin.SetId(pin_id)

        try:
            pin_name = normalize_pin_name(pin.GetName())
        except Exception:
            continue

        location = get_pin_location(pin, pin_id)

        if pin_name not in pin_map:
            pin_map[pin_name] = []

        if location is not None:
            record = {"pin_id": pin_id, "sheet_id": location["sheet_id"], "x": location["x"], "y": location["y"]}
            pin_map[pin_name].append(record)

    return pin_map


#====================================================================================================================================
# FIND BASE CONNECTOR
#====================================================================================================================================
def find_base_connector_devices(connector_token, all_devices, dev):
    """Find a connector with the same naming rules as the working placer.

    After E3 creates schematic views, a connector may be exposed as W1.P1,
    W1.01, or W1.1#1 instead of the literal W1.1.  The old overlay only
    accepted an exact name, which wrongly rejected those valid connectors.
    Never match a similarly numbered connector belonging to another cable.
    """
    expected_name = connector_base_name(connector_token)
    expected_upper = expected_name.upper()
    expected_designation = normalize_connector_token(connector_token)
    root_name = CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME.upper()
    root_prefix = root_name + "."
    matches = []
    seen_ids = set()

    def add_match(record, reason):
        device_id = record["device_id"]
        if device_id not in seen_ids:
            matches.append(record)
            seen_ids.add(device_id)
            print("OVERLAY CONNECTOR MATCH:", expected_name,
                  "->", record["name"], "DEVICE ID:", device_id,
                  "MATCH TYPE:", reason)

    # Prefer the exact canonical name, but recognize the aliases already
    # supported by the main cable placer (P-prefix, leading zeroes, #suffix).
    for record in all_devices:
        device_name = clean_excel_value(record["name"])
        name_upper = device_name.upper()
        if name_upper == expected_upper:
            add_match(record, "EXACT")
        elif (name_upper.startswith(root_prefix)
              and normalize_connector_token(device_name) == expected_designation):
            add_match(record, "NORMALIZED")

    # E3 can report an assembly member's LOCAL name (1/P1) rather than
    # W1.1.  Accept those only if its actual parent assembly is W1.
    if not matches:
        for record in all_devices:
            device_name = clean_excel_value(record["name"])
            if normalize_connector_token(device_name) != expected_designation:
                continue
            if device_name.upper().startswith(root_prefix):
                continue
            try:
                dev.SetId(record["device_id"])
                assembly_id = int(dev.GetAssemblyId())
                if assembly_id <= 0:
                    continue
                dev.SetId(assembly_id)
                if clean_excel_value(dev.GetName()).upper() == root_name:
                    add_match(record, "ASSEMBLY MEMBER")
            except Exception as error:
                print("OVERLAY LOCAL-NAME MATCH FAILED:", device_name, repr(error))

    # On some E3 versions the assembly children aren't included in
    # GetAllDeviceIds.  Explicitly enumerate children of the W1 assembly.
    if not matches:
        for root_record in all_devices:
            if clean_excel_value(root_record["name"]).upper() != root_name:
                continue
            for child_id in get_child_device_ids(dev, root_record["device_id"]):
                try:
                    dev.SetId(child_id)
                    child_name = clean_excel_value(dev.GetName())
                except Exception:
                    continue
                if normalize_connector_token(child_name) == expected_designation:
                    add_match({"device_id": child_id, "name": child_name},
                              "W1 ASSEMBLY CHILD")

    if not matches:
        cable_names = [record["name"] for record in all_devices
                       if clean_excel_value(record["name"]).upper().startswith(root_prefix)]
        print("OVERLAY LOOKUP FAILED FOR:", expected_name)
        print("AVAILABLE", root_name, "CONNECTORS:", cable_names)

    return matches


#====================================================================================================================================
# GET VIEW IDS
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_device_view_ids(dev, base_device_id):
    dev.SetId(base_device_id)

    if not hasattr(dev, "GetViewIds"):
        print("GetViewIds IS NOT AVAILABLE ON THIS E3 DEVICE OBJECT")
        return []

    try:
        result = dev.GetViewIds(None)
    except Exception as error:
        print("GetViewIds FAILED FOR BASE ID:", base_device_id, "ERROR:", repr(error))
        return []

    view_ids = extract_e3_ids(result)

    print("BASE DEVICE ID:", base_device_id, "VIEW IDS:", view_ids)

    return view_ids


#====================================================================================================================================
# GET CHILD DEVICE IDS
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_child_device_ids(dev, base_device_id):
    dev.SetId(base_device_id)

    if not hasattr(dev, "GetDeviceIds"):
        return []

    try:
        result = dev.GetDeviceIds(None)
    except Exception:
        return []

    return extract_e3_ids(result)


#====================================================================================================================================
# GET SAME NAME ASSEMBLY DEVICES
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_same_name_assembly_devices(dev, base_device_id, expected_name):
    found_ids = []

    dev.SetId(base_device_id)

    try:
        assembly_id = dev.GetAssemblyId()
    except Exception:
        return found_ids

    if assembly_id is None:
        return found_ids

    try:
        assembly_id = int(assembly_id)
    except Exception:
        return found_ids

    if assembly_id <= 0:
        return found_ids

    dev.SetId(assembly_id)

    try:
        device_info = dev.GetDeviceIds(None)
    except Exception:
        return found_ids

    device_ids = extract_e3_ids(device_info)

    for device_id in device_ids:
        dev.SetId(device_id)

        try:
            name = clean_excel_value(dev.GetName())
        except Exception:
            continue

        if (normalize_connector_token(name) == normalize_connector_token(expected_name)
                and (name.upper().startswith(CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME.upper() + ".")
                     or name.upper() == expected_name.upper())):
            found_ids.append(device_id)

    return found_ids


#====================================================================================================================================
# COLLECT CONNECTOR CANDIDATES
#====================================================================================================================================
def collect_connector_candidate_ids(connector_token, all_devices, dev):
    expected_name = connector_base_name(connector_token)
    base_records = find_base_connector_devices(connector_token, all_devices, dev)

    if not base_records:
        root_prefix = CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME.upper() + "."
        visible_names = [record["name"] for record in all_devices
                         if clean_excel_value(record["name"]).upper().startswith(root_prefix)]
        available = ", ".join(visible_names[:15]) if visible_names else "none enumerated"
        raise Exception(
            f"Could not find E3 connector/view for {expected_name}. "
            f"E3 currently reports these {CONNECTOR_ROOT_NAME} device names: {available}. "
            "Check that the selected workbook matches the open E3 cable "
            "and that its connector pin views have been placed."
        )

    candidate_sources = {}

    for record in base_records:
        base_id = record["device_id"]

        candidate_sources[base_id] = "BASE"

        view_ids = get_device_view_ids(dev, base_id)

        for view_id in view_ids:
            candidate_sources[view_id] = "VIEW"

        child_ids = get_child_device_ids(dev, base_id)

        for child_id in child_ids:
            if child_id not in candidate_sources:
                candidate_sources[child_id] = "CHILD"

        assembly_ids = get_same_name_assembly_devices(dev, base_id, expected_name)

        for assembly_device_id in assembly_ids:
            if assembly_device_id not in candidate_sources:
                candidate_sources[assembly_device_id] = "ASSEMBLY"

    return candidate_sources


#====================================================================================================================================
# ANALYZE CANDIDATE VIEW
#====================================================================================================================================
def analyze_candidate(required_pins, pin_map):
    sheets = {}

    for pin_name in required_pins:
        locations = pin_map.get(pin_name, [])

        for location in locations:
            sheet_id = location["sheet_id"]

            if sheet_id not in sheets:
                sheets[sheet_id] = {"pins": set(), "xs": [], "ys": []}

            sheets[sheet_id]["pins"].add(pin_name)
            sheets[sheet_id]["xs"].append(location["x"])
            sheets[sheet_id]["ys"].append(location["y"])

    if not sheets:
        return None

    results = []

    for sheet_id, data in sheets.items():
        placed_count = len(data["pins"])
        unique_x_values = set(round(value, COORDINATE_ROUND_DIGITS) for value in data["xs"])
        unique_y_values = set(round(value, COORDINATE_ROUND_DIGITS) for value in data["ys"])
        unique_x_count = len(unique_x_values)
        unique_y_count = len(unique_y_values)
        x_span = max(data["xs"]) - min(data["xs"]) if data["xs"] else 0.0
        y_span = max(data["ys"]) - min(data["ys"]) if data["ys"] else 0.0
        has_real_y_spread = y_span >= SCHEMATIC_MIN_Y_SPAN and unique_y_count > 1
        result = {"sheet_id": sheet_id, "placed_count": placed_count, "unique_x_count": unique_x_count, "unique_y_count": unique_y_count, "x_span": x_span, "y_span": y_span, "has_real_y_spread": has_real_y_spread}

        results.append(result)

    results.sort(key=lambda item: (item["has_real_y_spread"], item["placed_count"], item["unique_y_count"], item["y_span"]), reverse=True)

    return results[0]


#====================================================================================================================================
# RESOLVE REAL SCHEMATIC VIEW
#====================================================================================================================================
def resolve_connector_schematic_view(connector_token, required_pins, all_devices, dev, pin, cache):
    expected_name = connector_base_name(connector_token)
    candidate_sources = collect_connector_candidate_ids(connector_token, all_devices, dev)
    candidates = []

    print("")
    print("============================================================")
    print("E3 CANDIDATES FOR:", expected_name)
    print("============================================================")

    for candidate_id, source in candidate_sources.items():
        dev.SetId(candidate_id)

        try:
            candidate_name = clean_excel_value(dev.GetName())
        except Exception:
            candidate_name = "UNKNOWN"

        if candidate_id not in cache:
            try:
                cache[candidate_id] = load_device_pin_map(dev, pin, candidate_id)
            except Exception as error:
                print("CANDIDATE ID:", candidate_id, "SOURCE:", source, "NAME:", candidate_name, "PIN MAP FAILED:", repr(error))
                continue

        pin_map = cache[candidate_id]
        analysis = analyze_candidate(required_pins, pin_map)

        if analysis is None:
            print("CANDIDATE ID:", candidate_id, "SOURCE:", source, "NAME:", candidate_name, "NO PLACED REQUIRED PINS")
            continue

        print("CANDIDATE ID:", candidate_id, "SOURCE:", source, "NAME:", candidate_name, "SHEET:", analysis["sheet_id"], "PLACED:", analysis["placed_count"], "UNIQUE X:", analysis["unique_x_count"], "UNIQUE Y:", analysis["unique_y_count"], "X SPAN:", analysis["x_span"], "Y SPAN:", analysis["y_span"])

        candidate = {"device_id": candidate_id, "name": candidate_name, "source": source, "pin_map": pin_map, "analysis": analysis}

        candidates.append(candidate)

    if not candidates:
        raise Exception(f"No usable E3 device/view candidates found for {expected_name}")

    candidates.sort(key=lambda item: (item["analysis"]["has_real_y_spread"], item["analysis"]["placed_count"], item["analysis"]["unique_y_count"], item["analysis"]["y_span"]), reverse=True)

    best = candidates[0]

    if len(required_pins) == 1:
        # A connector may have just ONE wire within its own outer shield.
        # One pin cannot establish vertical spread, so select a placed
        # schematic VIEW over an assembly/base-device candidate when possible.
        # Keep the original multi-pin view validation unchanged.
        view_priority = {"VIEW": 3, "CHILD": 2, "ASSEMBLY": 1, "BASE": 0}
        candidates.sort(
            key=lambda item: (
                view_priority.get(item["source"], 0),
                item["analysis"]["placed_count"],
            ),
            reverse=True,
        )
        best = candidates[0]
        print("SINGLE-PIN SHIELD VIEW: using placed-pin sheet without requiring Y spread")
    elif not best["analysis"]["has_real_y_spread"]:
        raise Exception(f"Could not locate the split schematic view for {expected_name}")

    sheet_id = best["analysis"]["sheet_id"]
    missing_pins = []

    for pin_name in required_pins:
        locations = best["pin_map"].get(pin_name, [])
        found_on_sheet = False

        for location in locations:
            if location["sheet_id"] == sheet_id:
                found_on_sheet = True
                break

        if not found_on_sheet:
            missing_pins.append(pin_name)

    if missing_pins:
        raise Exception(f"Selected schematic view for {expected_name} is missing pins on sheet {sheet_id}: {missing_pins}")

    print("")
    print("SELECTED SCHEMATIC VIEW ID:", best["device_id"])
    print("SELECTED SOURCE:", best["source"])
    print("SELECTED NAME:", best["name"])
    print("SELECTED SHEET:", sheet_id)
    print("SELECTED PLACED PIN COUNT:", best["analysis"]["placed_count"])
    print("SELECTED UNIQUE Y COUNT:", best["analysis"]["unique_y_count"])
    print("SELECTED Y SPAN:", best["analysis"]["y_span"])
    print("============================================================")

    record = {"device_id": best["device_id"], "name": best["name"]}

    return record, best["pin_map"], sheet_id


#====================================================================================================================================
# PIN LOCATION ON SELECTED SHEET
#====================================================================================================================================
def pin_location_on_sheet(pin_map, pin_name, sheet_id):
    locations = pin_map.get(pin_name, [])

    for location in locations:
        if location["sheet_id"] == sheet_id:
            return location

    return None


#====================================================================================================================================
# PRINT PIN LOCATIONS
#====================================================================================================================================
def print_section_pin_locations(required_pins, pin_map, sheet_id):
    print("")
    print("ACTUAL SCHEMATIC PIN LOCATIONS")

    for pin_name in required_pins:
        location = pin_location_on_sheet(pin_map, pin_name, sheet_id)

        if location is None:
            print("PIN:", pin_name, "LOCATION: NOT FOUND")
            continue

        print("PIN:", pin_name, "X:", location["x"], "Y:", location["y"])


#====================================================================================================================================
# JOB X OFFSET
#====================================================================================================================================
def job_offset(job, twist_offset_settings):
    offset_enabled = twist_offset_settings[job["side"]]

    if job["type"] == "twist":
        if not offset_enabled:
            return OFFSET_NO_TWIST_LINE

        if job["twist_column"] == "A":
            return OFFSET_YES_TWIST_A

        return OFFSET_YES_TWIST_B

    if job["type"] == "shield_inner":
        if job["twist_column"] is None:
            return INNER_SHIELD_ONLY_OFFSET

        if not offset_enabled:
            return OFFSET_NO_TWIST_LINE

        if job["twist_column"] == "A":
            return OFFSET_YES_TWIST_A

        return OFFSET_YES_TWIST_B

    if not offset_enabled:
        return OFFSET_NO_OUTER_SHIELD

    return OFFSET_YES_OUTER_SHIELD


#====================================================================================================================================
# DRAW ORDER
#
# ACTUAL WORKING ORDER INSIDE EACH CONNECTOR SECTION:
# Inner shields first, then twists.
# Connector-specific outer shields are processed before inner shields/twists.
#====================================================================================================================================
def job_priority(job):
    # This matches the earlier working dynamic-placement implementation:
    # shields are created before twists.
    if job["type"] == "shield_outer":
        return 0

    if job["type"] == "shield_inner":
        return 1

    return 2


#====================================================================================================================================
# CALCULATE FINAL SYMBOL PLACEMENT
#====================================================================================================================================
def calculate_placement(job, pin_map, sheet_id, twist_offset_settings):
    locations = []

    for pin_name in job["pins"]:
        location = pin_location_on_sheet(pin_map, pin_name, sheet_id)

        if location is None:
            raise Exception(f"{job['side']} {job['connector']}: pin {pin_name} has no schematic location on sheet {sheet_id}")

        locations.append(location)

    x_values = [location["x"] for location in locations]
    y_values = [location["y"] for location in locations]

    anchor_x = statistics.median(x_values)
    bottom_y = min(y_values)
    top_y = max(y_values)

    if top_y - bottom_y < SCHEMATIC_MIN_Y_SPAN and len(job["pins"]) > 1:
        raise Exception(f"{job['side']} {job['connector']} {job['type']}: pins {job['pins']} all have effectively the same Y coordinate")

    direction = 1.0 if job["side"] == "LEFT" else -1.0
    offset = job_offset(job, twist_offset_settings)
    x = anchor_x + direction * offset

    if job["type"] == "twist":
        y = bottom_y - TWIST_BOTTOM_OVERLAP
        length = None
    else:
        y = bottom_y - SHIELD_BOTTOM_OVERLAP
        length = top_y - bottom_y + SHIELD_BOTTOM_OVERLAP + SHIELD_TOP_OVERLAP

        if length < MIN_SYMBOL_LENGTH:
            length = MIN_SYMBOL_LENGTH

    placement = {
        "sheet_id": sheet_id,
        "x": x,
        "y": y,
        "length": length,
        "anchor_x": anchor_x,
        "bottom_y": bottom_y,
        "top_y": top_y,
        "direction": direction,
        "offset_enabled": twist_offset_settings[job["side"]]
    }

    return placement



#====================================================================================================================================
# CALCULATE ONE CONNECTOR'S OUTER SHIELD PLACEMENT
#
# The job builder guarantees every row belongs to the same connector.
# Resolve that connector's actual placed pins and size Shield_Demo only
# from its first to its last marked wire.  The existing E3 sizing and
# placement workflow below is intentionally unchanged.
#====================================================================================================================================
def calculate_outer_shield_placement(job, all_devices, dev, pin, cache, twist_offset_settings):
    resolved_by_connector = {}
    sheet_ids = set()
    locations = []

    rows_by_connector = {}

    for row in job["rows"]:
        connector = row["connector"]

        if connector == "":
            raise Exception(
                f"{job['side']} outer shield: Excel row {row['excel_row']} has no connector"
            )

        rows_by_connector.setdefault(connector, []).append(row)

    for connector, connector_rows in rows_by_connector.items():
        required_pins = list(dict.fromkeys(row["pin"] for row in connector_rows))

        device_record, pin_map, sheet_id = resolve_connector_schematic_view(
            connector,
            required_pins,
            all_devices,
            dev,
            pin,
            cache
        )

        resolved_by_connector[connector] = {
            "device_record": device_record,
            "pin_map": pin_map,
            "sheet_id": sheet_id
        }

        sheet_ids.add(sheet_id)

    if len(sheet_ids) != 1:
        details = ", ".join(
            f"{connector}=sheet {data['sheet_id']}"
            for connector, data in resolved_by_connector.items()
        )
        raise Exception(
            f"{job['side']} outer shield spans connectors on different E3 sheets: {details}"
        )

    sheet_id = next(iter(sheet_ids))

    for row in job["rows"]:
        connector = row["connector"]
        pin_name = row["pin"]
        pin_map = resolved_by_connector[connector]["pin_map"]

        location = pin_location_on_sheet(pin_map, pin_name, sheet_id)

        if location is None:
            raise Exception(
                f"{job['side']} outer shield: {connector} pin {pin_name} "
                f"has no schematic location on sheet {sheet_id}"
            )

        locations.append(location)

    if not locations:
        raise Exception(f"{job['side']} outer shield has no resolved schematic locations")

    x_values = [location["x"] for location in locations]
    y_values = [location["y"] for location in locations]

    anchor_x = statistics.median(x_values)
    bottom_y = min(y_values)
    top_y = max(y_values)

    direction = 1.0 if job["side"] == "LEFT" else -1.0

    if twist_offset_settings[job["side"]]:
        offset = OFFSET_YES_OUTER_SHIELD
    else:
        offset = OFFSET_NO_OUTER_SHIELD

    x = anchor_x + direction * offset
    y = bottom_y - SHIELD_BOTTOM_OVERLAP
    length = top_y - bottom_y + SHIELD_BOTTOM_OVERLAP + SHIELD_TOP_OVERLAP

    if length < MIN_SYMBOL_LENGTH:
        length = MIN_SYMBOL_LENGTH

    return {
        "sheet_id": sheet_id,
        "x": x,
        "y": y,
        "length": length,
        "anchor_x": anchor_x,
        "bottom_y": bottom_y,
        "top_y": top_y,
        "direction": direction,
        "offset_enabled": twist_offset_settings[job["side"]]
    }


#====================================================================================================================================
# SAFE DISPLAY LENGTH READ
#====================================================================================================================================
def get_display_length_safe(sym, symbol_id):
    sym.SetId(symbol_id)

    try:
        return float(sym.GetDisplayLength())
    except Exception:
        return None


#====================================================================================================================================
# TRY TO SIZE SHIELD AT CURRENT LOCATION
#====================================================================================================================================
def try_size_shield(sym, symbol_id, requested_length):
    # EXACT KNOWN-GOOD E3 SEQUENCE:
    #   Place -> SetId(placed id) -> GetDisplayLength ->
    #   SetDisplayLength -> SetId(placed id) -> GetDisplayLength
    sym.SetId(symbol_id)

    try:
        before = float(sym.GetDisplayLength())
    except Exception:
        before = None

    print("SHIELD LENGTH BEFORE:", before)

    try:
        stretch_result = sym.SetDisplayLength(float(requested_length))
    except Exception as error:
        stretch_result = None
        print("SHIELD STRETCH EXCEPTION:", repr(error))

    print("SHIELD STRETCH RESULT:", stretch_result)

    # Re-bind exactly like the standalone test that successfully resized.
    sym.SetId(symbol_id)
    time.sleep(STRETCH_DELAY_SECONDS)

    try:
        after = float(sym.GetDisplayLength())
    except Exception:
        after = None

    print("SHIELD REQUESTED LENGTH:", requested_length)
    print("SHIELD LENGTH AFTER:", after)

    return after is not None and abs(after - float(requested_length)) <= 0.001


def shield_stage_y_values(requested_length):
    # Shield_Demo grows upward from its insertion point.
    # 10.0 at Y=22.0 was proven to resize successfully in the earlier test.
    # For longer shields, lower the staging Y enough to keep the full symbol on-sheet.
    safe_y = min(
        SHIELD_STAGE_DEFAULT_Y,
        SHIELD_STAGE_TOP_LIMIT - float(requested_length) - SHIELD_STAGE_MARGIN
    )
    safe_y = max(0.75, safe_y)

    candidates = [
        safe_y,
        max(0.75, safe_y - 0.50),
        min(SHIELD_STAGE_DEFAULT_Y, safe_y + 0.50),
        max(0.75, safe_y - 1.00),
        min(SHIELD_STAGE_DEFAULT_Y, safe_y + 1.00),
    ]

    output = []
    for value in candidates:
        value = round(float(value), 3)
        if value not in output:
            output.append(value)

    return output


#====================================================================================================================================
# PLACE TWIST
#
# NO STRETCH.
#
# E3/database decides display length automatically.
#====================================================================================================================================
def place_twist(sym, job, placement):
    load_result = sym.Load(job["symbol_name"], SYMBOL_VERSION)

    print("TWIST LOAD RESULT:", load_result)

    if load_result == 0:
        raise Exception(f"Could not load library twist {job['symbol_name']}")

    print("TWIST TARGET X:", placement["x"])
    print("TWIST TARGET Y:", placement["y"])

    placed_id = sym.Place(placement["sheet_id"], placement["x"], placement["y"], 0, 1.0, 0)

    print("TWIST PLACE RESULT:", placed_id)

    if placed_id == 0:
        raise Exception(f"Could not place library twist {job['symbol_name']} at X={placement['x']} Y={placement['y']}")

    time.sleep(PLACEMENT_DELAY_SECONDS)

    sym.SetId(placed_id)

    try:
        placed_name = sym.GetName()
    except Exception:
        placed_name = "UNKNOWN"

    try:
        symbol_type = sym.GetSymbolType()
    except Exception:
        symbol_type = "UNKNOWN"

    try:
        automatic_length = float(sym.GetDisplayLength())
    except Exception:
        automatic_length = None

    print("TWIST PLACED ID:", placed_id)
    print("TWIST PLACED NAME:", placed_name)
    print("TWIST SYMBOL TYPE:", symbol_type)
    print("TWIST AUTOMATIC DISPLAY LENGTH:", automatic_length)
    print("TWIST LEFT AT E3 DATABASE AUTO LENGTH")

    return placed_id


# ======================================================================
# SHIELD PLACEMENT BACKPORT
# ======================================================================
# E3 symbol Load / Place / immediate SetDisplayLength and stage->move were
# restored from zuken_cable_overlay_v9_exact_working_core.py.
# Changes retained elsewhere: D15 0.20/0.25 (blank -> 0.20), no-offset
# outside twist position, connector aliases / pin view discovery, GUI.
# Extremely tall shields are staged only at valid on-page Y coordinates.
# Failed automatic stretches report failure instead of silently leaving an
# incorrectly-sized (native-length) shield.
# ======================================================================

#====================================================================================================================================
# PLACE SHIELD
#
# PROCESS:
#
# 1. Load Shield_Demo.
# 2. Search staging coordinates until the symbol can be placed.
# 3. Try SetDisplayLength at that staging position.
# 4. If resize fails, move SAME shield to another staging location.
# 5. Once correct size is reached, move SAME shield to final cable X/Y.
#
# No duplicate shields are intentionally created while searching.
