''',
    'block_maker/utils.py': r'''import time
import json
import os
import win32com.client as win32


def format_current_secondary_pin_views(prj, metadata_path, modified_texts):
    """Format the CURRENT E3 pin-view symbols, not PlacePins' stale IDs.

    Based on the working Pin Arranger approach: Pin.GetViewIds and the
    split-position text type 213. E3 may rebuild earlier END_SPLIT symbols
    while placing later pins, invalidating the IDs returned by PlacePins.
    If Pin.GetViewIds is unavailable, recover by inspecting the symbols on
    the connector's actual sheet, matching designation + pin number + position.
    This function changes TEXT ONLY; never re-places symbols or changes nets.
    """
    import re

    if not metadata_path or not os.path.isfile(metadata_path):
        print("PIN TEXT FORMAT: No secondary metadata available", flush=True)
        return
    try:
        with open(metadata_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            blocks = json.load(handle)
    except Exception as exc:
        print("PIN TEXT FORMAT: Could not read metadata:", repr(exc), flush=True)
        return

    dev_obj = prj.CreateDeviceObject()
    pin_obj = prj.CreatePinObject()
    sym_obj = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
    text_obj = prj.CreateTextObject()
    sht_obj = prj.CreateSheetObject()

    def ids(result):
        if isinstance(result, (tuple, list)):
            if len(result) >= 2 and isinstance(result[1], (tuple, list)):
                result = result[1]
            elif all(type(x) is int for x in result):
                pass
            else:
                return []
            return [x for x in result if type(x) is int and x > 0]
        return [result] if type(result) is int and result > 0 else []

    def call_ids(obj, method):
        fn = getattr(obj, method, None)
        if fn is None:
            return []
        for args in ((None,), ()):
            try:
                return ids(fn(*args))
            except Exception:
                pass
        return []

    def clean(value):
        return str(value or "").strip().casefold()

    def same_pin(actual, expected):
        actual, expected = clean(actual), clean(expected)
        return (actual == expected or
                actual.split("-", 1)[0] == expected or
                expected.split("-", 1)[0] == actual)

    cache = {}

    def snap(symbol_id):
        if symbol_id in cache:
            return cache[symbol_id]
        try:
            sym_obj.SetId(symbol_id)
            library = str(sym_obj.GetSymbolTypeName() or "").strip()
            if not library.lower().startswith("milblock"):
                cache[symbol_id] = None
                return None
            items = []
            for tid in call_ids(sym_obj, "GetTextIds"):
                try:
                    text_obj.SetId(tid)
                    items.append((int(text_obj.GetType()),
                                  str(text_obj.GetText() or "").strip(), tid))
                except Exception:
                    continue
            positions = []
            for typ, val, _ in items:
                if typ == 213:
                    match = re.fullmatch(r"\s*(\d+)\s*/\s*(\d+)\s*", val)
                    if match:
                        positions.append(int(match.group(1)))
            data = {"id": symbol_id, "library": library, "items": items,
                    "position": positions[0] if len(positions) == 1 else None}
            cache[symbol_id] = data
            return data
        except Exception:
            cache[symbol_id] = None
            return None

    def values(candidate, typ):
        return [val for t, val, _ in candidate["items"] if t == typ]

    def belongs(candidate, connector):
        # E3 assigns view suffixes dynamically (#1, #2, ...) when multiple
        # connectors share the main device view. Never hardcode #1.
        # Compare the COMPLETE connector designation before the suffix so
        # J1 cannot accidentally match X-J1 or another connector of the
        # same part number. The prefix may include E3 location text.
        expected = clean(connector)
        for val in values(candidate, 12):
            last = clean(val).split()[-1] if clean(val) else ""
            match = re.fullmatch(r"(.+?)#\d+", last)
            if match and match.group(1).lstrip("-") == expected:
                return True
        return False

    def qualifies(candidate, connector, pin_name, position):
        if not candidate:
            return False
        if candidate["position"] is not None and candidate["position"] != position:
            return False
        num_matches = any(same_pin(n, pin_name) for n in values(candidate, 203))
        if not num_matches:
            return False
        return belongs(candidate, connector)

    def pin_views(pin_id):
        try:
            pin_obj.SetId(pin_id)
            return call_ids(pin_obj, "GetViewIds")
        except Exception:
            return []

    def get_current_pin_ids(block):
        mapping = {}
        view_id = block.get("placed_pin_symbols", [{}])[0].get("connector_view_id")
        if view_id:
            try:
                dev_obj.SetId(int(view_id))
                for pid in call_ids(dev_obj, "GetAllPinIds"):
                    try:
                        pin_obj.SetId(pid)
                        mapping[str(pin_obj.GetName()).strip()] = pid
                    except Exception:
                        pass
            except Exception:
                pass
        return mapping

    def get_sheet_symbols(sheet_id):
        if sheet_id:
            try:
                sht_obj.SetId(int(sheet_id))
                result = call_ids(sht_obj, "GetSymbolIds")
                if result:
                    return result
            except Exception:
                pass
        return call_ids(prj, "GetSymbolIds")

    def set_and_check(method, value, getter):
        try:
            getattr(text_obj, method)(value)
            observed = getattr(text_obj, getter)()
            if getter == "GetHeight":
                return (isinstance(observed, (int, float)) and
                        abs(float(observed) - float(value)) < 0.0001), observed
            return observed == value, observed
        except Exception as exc:
            return False, repr(exc)

    print("\n" + "=" * 60, flush=True)
    print("PIN ARRANGER-STYLE CURRENT SECONDARY PIN FORMATTING", flush=True)
    print("Resolving live views, not the original PlacePins symbol IDs", flush=True)
    print("=" * 60, flush=True)

    total_expected = total_resolved = total_failed = 0
    total_text_success = total_text_fail = 0
    seen_symbols = set()
    for block in blocks:
        connector = str(block.get("name", "")).strip()
        entries = block.get("placed_pin_symbols", [])
        if not entries:
            continue
        current_pin_ids = get_current_pin_ids(block)
        sheet_symbols = get_sheet_symbols(block.get("sheet_id"))
        print(f"CONNECTOR {connector}: {len(entries)} pins; sheet candidates={len(sheet_symbols)}", flush=True)

        for entry in entries:
            total_expected += 1
            name = str(entry["pin_name"]).strip()
            pos = int(entry["position"])
            old_id = int(entry["symbol_id"])
            possible_pids = [int(entry["pin_id"])]
            for current_name, current_pid in current_pin_ids.items():
                if same_pin(current_name, name) and current_pid not in possible_pids:
                    possible_pids.append(current_pid)

            # Try the Pin Arranger's live Pin.GetViewIds path first.
            via_pin = []
            for pid in possible_pids:
                via_pin.extend(pin_views(pid))
            via_pin = list(dict.fromkeys(via_pin))
            matches = [snap(sid) for sid in via_pin]
            matches = [s for s in matches if qualifies(s, connector, name, pos)]

            # Some E3 builds don't return newly split symbols from GetViewIds.
            # Enumerate placed symbols on the *same sheet* as a safe fallback.
            if not matches:
                matches = [snap(sid) for sid in sheet_symbols]
                matches = [s for s in matches if qualifies(s, connector, name, pos)]

            # Never silently format the wrong pin if ownership is ambiguous.
            matches = {s["id"]: s for s in matches}.values()
            matches = list(matches)
            if len(matches) != 1:
                total_failed += 1
                print(f"  FAIL pin {name} position {pos}: found {len(matches)} current matching symbols; "
                      f"old PlacePins ID={old_id}, Pin.GetViewIds={via_pin}", flush=True)
                continue

            current = matches[0]
            sid = current["id"]
            if sid in seen_symbols:
                total_failed += 1
                print(f"  FAIL pin {name}: current symbol {sid} already assigned to another pin", flush=True)
                continue
            seen_symbols.add(sid)
            total_resolved += 1
            print(f"  PIN {name} position {pos}: old ID {old_id} -> CURRENT {sid} "
                  f"({current['library']})", flush=True)

            # Every split-pin symbol may carry its own Location text (type 15),
            # especially the receptacle family. Hide Location on ALL pin views.
            required = {5, 15, 617, 203}
            if pos == 1:
                required.add(12)
            present = {typ for typ, _, _ in current["items"]}
            for missing in sorted(required - present):
                print(f"    WARNING: expected text type {missing} not present on current symbol", flush=True)

            for typ, val, tid in current["items"]:
                if typ not in (5, 12, 15, 617, 203):
                    continue
                # 5: component code off on ALL pins
                # 12: top designation visible, bold, 0.2"; others hidden
                # 15: Location OFF on EVERY secondary pin (plug + receptacle)
                # 617: local signal visible, black, bold, 0.15"
                # 203: pin number visible and bold
                desired_visibility = 0 if (typ in (5, 15) or (typ == 12 and pos != 1)) else 1
                text_obj.SetId(tid)
                checks = []
                ok, actual = set_and_check("SetVisibility", desired_visibility, "GetVisibility")
                checks.append(ok)
                if typ == 12 and pos == 1:
                    checks.append(set_and_check("SetStyle", 1, "GetStyle")[0])
                    checks.append(set_and_check("SetHeight", 0.2, "GetHeight")[0])
                    checks.append(set_and_check("SetColour", 0, "GetColour")[0])
                elif typ == 617:
                    checks.append(set_and_check("SetStyle", 1, "GetStyle")[0])
                    checks.append(set_and_check("SetHeight", 0.15, "GetHeight")[0])
                    checks.append(set_and_check("SetColour", 0, "GetColour")[0])
                    try:
                        text_obj.SetAlignment(3)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                elif typ == 203:
                    checks.append(set_and_check("SetStyle", 1, "GetStyle")[0])

                if all(checks):
                    total_text_success += 1
                else:
                    total_text_fail += 1
                # Readback proves the glyph, not just the logical pin, changed.
                try:
                    final_style = text_obj.GetStyle()
                except Exception:
                    final_style = "N/A"
                try:
                    final_height = text_obj.GetHeight()
                except Exception:
                    final_height = "N/A"
                print(f"    TYPE {typ} {val!r}: visible={actual!r}/{desired_visibility} "
                      f"style={final_style!r} height={final_height!r} "
                      f"{'PASS' if all(checks) else 'FAIL'}", flush=True)
                modified_texts.append({
                    "device": connector, "pin": name, "position": pos,
                    "view": sid, "text_id": tid, "type": typ,
                    "value": val, "desired": desired_visibility,
                })
    print(f"PIN VIEW RESOLUTION: {total_resolved}/{total_expected} pins; unresolved={total_failed}", flush=True)
    print(f"PIN TEXT READBACK: PASS={total_text_success} FAIL={total_text_fail}", flush=True)
    if total_failed or total_text_fail:
        print("WARNING: Some pin text was NOT verified; see lines above", flush=True)


def format_current_main_connector_symbols(prj, metadata_path, modified_texts, text_size=0.2):
    """Format every current FULL connector on the main drawing, not just one.

    Important when there are multiple connectors: metadata `sheet_id` is the
    SECONDARY schematic-block sheet, not necessarily the TOP-LEVEL connector
    sheet. E3 can also replace a symbol ID while rearranging MIL views.

    Resolve current FULL symbols across ALL project sheets, using their current
    type-12 device designation and, if present, the original connector device's
    GetSymbolIds() as ownership evidence. Never edit an ambiguous match.
    Only text styling/visibility changes; no pin/device placement or nets.
    """
    if not metadata_path or not os.path.isfile(metadata_path):
        print("MAIN CONNECTOR FORMAT: No metadata available", flush=True)
        return
    try:
        with open(metadata_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            blocks = json.load(handle)
    except Exception as exc:
        print("MAIN CONNECTOR FORMAT: Metadata read error:", repr(exc), flush=True)
        return

    sym_obj = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
    text_obj = prj.CreateTextObject()
    sheet_obj = prj.CreateSheetObject()
    dev_obj = prj.CreateDeviceObject()

    def ids(result):
        if isinstance(result, (tuple, list)):
            if len(result) >= 2 and isinstance(result[1], (list, tuple)):
                result = result[1]
            elif not all(type(x) is int for x in result):
                return []
            return [x for x in result if type(x) is int and x > 0]
        return [result] if type(result) is int and result > 0 else []

    def get_ids(obj, method):
        func = getattr(obj, method, None)
        if func is None:
            return []
        for args in ((None,), ()):
            try:
                return ids(func(*args))
            except Exception:
                continue
        return []

    def clean(s):
        return str(s or "").strip().casefold().lstrip("-")

    def name_in_designation(display, name):
        """Match FULL connector designation, not a secondary '-J1#1' label."""
        v = clean(display)
        expected = clean(name)
        if not v or not expected or "#" in v.rsplit(None, 1)[-1]:
            return False
        if v == expected:
            return True
        return v.split()[-1].lstrip("-") == expected

    def text_set(text_id, setter, getter, desired):
        text_obj.SetId(text_id)
        try:
            result = getattr(text_obj, setter)(desired)
            actual = getattr(text_obj, getter)()
            ok = (isinstance(actual, (int, float)) and
                  abs(float(actual) - float(desired)) < 0.0001) if getter == "GetHeight" else actual == desired
            if result is False or (type(result) is int and result < 0):
                ok = False
            return ok, actual
        except Exception as exc:
            return False, repr(exc)

    # Across all sheets, because secondary-block sheet != main connector sheet.
    sheet_ids = get_ids(prj, "GetAllSheetIds") or get_ids(prj, "GetSheetIds")
    all_ids = set(get_ids(prj, "GetSymbolIds"))
    for sheet_id in sheet_ids:
        try:
            sheet_obj.SetId(sheet_id)
            all_ids.update(get_ids(sheet_obj, "GetSymbolIds"))
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"MAIN FORMAT: cannot scan sheet {sheet_id}: {exc!r}", flush=True)

    # Also include direct symbols from the connector's original DEVICE ID.
    device_symbol_ids = {}
    for block in blocks:
        cname = str(block.get("name", "")).strip()
        original_device_ids = {
            int(x["parent_connector_id"])
            for x in block.get("placed_pin_symbols", [])
            if type(x.get("parent_connector_id")) is int and x["parent_connector_id"] > 0
        }
        associated = set()
        for did in original_device_ids:
            try:
                dev_obj.SetId(did)
                associated.update(get_ids(dev_obj, "GetSymbolIds"))
            except Exception:
                pass
        device_symbol_ids[cname] = associated
        all_ids.update(associated)

    # Snapshot valid, currently placed FULL MIL connectors exactly once.
    full = {}
    for sid in sorted(all_ids):
        try:
            sym_obj.SetId(sid)
            family = str(sym_obj.GetSymbolTypeName() or "").strip()
            if not family.startswith("MilBlock") or "¶FULL" not in family:
                continue
            items = []
            for tid in get_ids(sym_obj, "GetTextIds"):
                try:
                    text_obj.SetId(tid)
                    items.append((int(text_obj.GetType()),
                                  str(text_obj.GetText() or "").strip(), tid))
                except Exception:
                    continue
            if not any(typ == 5 for typ, _, _ in items):
                # Not a connector component-code symbol; don't touch it.
                continue
            full[sid] = (family, items)
        except Exception:
            continue

    print("\n" + "=" * 60, flush=True)
    print("MAIN CONNECTORS: MULTI-CONNECTOR CURRENT FULL SYMBOL FORMATTING", flush=True)
    print(f"Scanned {len(sheet_ids)} sheets; found {len(full)} FULL MIL connector symbols", flush=True)
    print("=" * 60, flush=True)

    matched = 0
    passed = failed = missing = 0
    used_symbols = set()
    for block in blocks:
        name = str(block.get("name", "")).strip()
        if not name:
            continue
        candidates = []
        direct_ids = device_symbol_ids.get(name, set())
        for sid, (family, items) in full.items():
            designations = [val for typ, val, _ in items if typ == 12]
            text_identity = any(name_in_designation(val, name) for val in designations)
            device_identity = sid in direct_ids
            if text_identity or device_identity:
                candidates.append((sid, family, items, text_identity, device_identity))

        # Device ownership is stronger than a rendered label, but never
        # silently choose among multiple actual FULL symbols.
        owned = [c for c in candidates if c[4]]
        if len(owned) == 1:
            candidates = owned
        elif len(owned) > 1:
            candidates = owned
        if len(candidates) != 1:
            missing += 1
            print(f"MAIN {name}: SKIP; {len(candidates)} unambiguous-match candidates "
                  f"(device-owned={len(owned)}, IDs={[c[0] for c in candidates]})", flush=True)
            continue
        sid, family, items, by_text, by_device = candidates[0]
        if sid in used_symbols:
            missing += 1
            print(f"MAIN {name}: SKIP; FULL symbol {sid} matched more than one connector", flush=True)
            continue
        used_symbols.add(sid)
        matched += 1
        print(f"MAIN {name}: CURRENT symbol {sid} ({family}), "
              f"match={'device' if by_device else 'designation'}", flush=True)

        for typ, value, tid in items:
            if typ not in (5, 12, 203):
                continue
            desired = 0 if typ == 5 else 1
            ops = [("SetVisibility", "GetVisibility", desired)]
            if typ == 12:
                ops += [("SetColour", "GetColour", 0),
                        ("SetStyle", "GetStyle", 1),
                        ("SetHeight", "GetHeight", float(text_size))]
            elif typ == 203:
                ops += [("SetColour", "GetColour", 0), ("SetStyle", "GetStyle", 1)]
            checks = [text_set(tid, *op) for op in ops]
            ok = all(result[0] for result in checks)
            if ok:
                passed += 1
            else:
                failed += 1
            print(f"    TYPE {typ} {value!r} ID={tid}, "
                  f"visible={checks[0][1]!r}/{desired}, "
                  f"{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}", flush=True)
            modified_texts.append({
                "device": name, "pin": "MAIN", "position": 0,
                "view": sid, "text_id": tid, "type": typ,
                "value": value, "desired": desired,
            })
    print(f"MAIN CONNECTOR TEXT: matched={matched}/{len(blocks)} "
          f"component-code/other text PASS={passed} FAIL={failed} "
          f"unmatched={missing}", flush=True)
    if missing or failed:
        print("WARNING: Check MAIN connector skips/failures above", flush=True)

def runn(secondary=False, TEXT_SIZE=0.25):
    # ================================================
    # CONNECT TO ACTIVE E3 PROJECT
    # ================================================
    app = win32.GetActiveObject("CT.Application")
    prj = app.CreateJobObject()
    dev = prj.CreateDeviceObject()
    pin = prj.CreatePinObject()
    sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
    text = prj.CreateTextObject()
    attr = prj.CreateAttributeObject()


    # ================================================
    # HELPERS
    # ================================================
    def extract_ids(result):
        if result is None:
            return []

        if isinstance(result, (tuple, list)):
            if len(result) >= 2 and isinstance(result[1], (tuple, list)):
                return [x for x in result[1] if x is not None and x != 0]

            return [x for x in result if isinstance(x, int) and x != 0]

        if isinstance(result, int):
            if result != 0:
                return [result]

        return []


    def get_visibility(text_id):
        text.SetId(text_id)

        try:
            return text.GetVisibility()
        except Exception as e:
            return "GET VIS ERROR: " + repr(e)


    def get_pin_view_ids(pin_id):
        pin.SetId(pin_id)

        try:
            result = pin.GetViewIds(None)
            return extract_ids(result)
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            result = pin.GetViewIds()
            return extract_ids(result)
        except Exception as e:
            print("GetViewIds ERROR:", pin_id, repr(e))
            return []


    def get_schema_location(text_id):
        text.SetId(text_id)

        xloc = [0.0]
        yloc = [0.0]
        grid = [""]
        column = [""]
        row = [""]

        try:
            text.GetSchemaLocation(xloc, yloc, grid, column, row)
            return float(xloc[0]), float(yloc[0])
        except Exception as e:
            print("GET SCHEMA LOCATION ERROR:", text_id, repr(e))
            return None, None


    def format_equipment_identification(TEXT_SIZE):
        print("")
        print("============================================================")
        print("FORMATTING EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION")
        print("USING EXACT BLOCK SYMBOL IDS FROM MAIN.PY")
        print("============================================================")

        metadata_path = os.environ.get("E3_SECONDARY_BLOCK_FILE")


        if not metadata_path:
            print("E3_SECONDARY_BLOCK_FILE WAS NOT PROVIDED BY LAUNCHER.PY")
            return 0

        if not os.path.isfile(metadata_path):
            print("SECONDARY BLOCK METADATA FILE NOT FOUND:", metadata_path)
            return 0

        try:
            with open(metadata_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as metadata_handle:
                secondary_blocks = json.load(metadata_handle)
        except Exception as e:
            print("FAILED TO READ SECONDARY BLOCK METADATA:", repr(e))
            return 0

        formatted_count = 0
        block_count = 0
        found_attribute_count = 0

        for block_data in secondary_blocks:
            block_count += 1

            block_name = str(block_data.get("name", ""))
            block_symbol_id = int(block_data["block_symbol_id"])
            parent_block = int(block_data["parent_block"])
            x = float(block_data["x"])
            y = float(block_data["y"])
            width = float(block_data["width"])
            height = float(block_data["height"])

            x_center = x + (width / 2.0)
            equipment_y = y + height - 1.1

            print("")
            print("SECONDARY BLOCK:", block_name, "VIEW:", parent_block, "SYMBOL:", block_symbol_id)

            try:
                sym.SetId(block_symbol_id)
            except Exception as e:
                print("FAILED TO SET BLOCK SYMBOL ID:", block_symbol_id, repr(e))
                continue

            try:
                attribute_result = sym.GetAttributeIds(None)
                attribute_ids = extract_ids(attribute_result)
            except Exception as e:
                print("FAILED TO GET BLOCK ATTRIBUTES:", block_symbol_id, repr(e))
                continue

            print("BLOCK ATTRIBUTE IDS:", attribute_ids)

            equipment_attribute_id = None

            for attribute_id in attribute_ids:
                attr.SetId(attribute_id)

                try:
                    attribute_name = str(attr.GetName()).strip()
                except Exception:
                    continue

                print("BLOCK ATTRIBUTE:", attribute_id, repr(attribute_name))

                if attribute_name == "Equipment Identifacation":
                    equipment_attribute_id = attribute_id
                    break

            if equipment_attribute_id is None:
                print("EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION NOT FOUND ON EXACT BLOCK SYMBOL:", block_symbol_id)
                continue

            found_attribute_count += 1
            attr.SetId(equipment_attribute_id)

            equipment_value = ""

            try:
                equipment_value = str(attr.GetValue()).strip()
            except Exception:
                try:
                    equipment_value = str(attr.GetFormattedValue()).strip()
                except Exception:
                    equipment_value = ""

            print("EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION VALUE:", repr(equipment_value))

            try:
                before_text_result = sym.GetTextIds(None)
                before_text_ids = extract_ids(before_text_result)
            except Exception:
                before_text_ids = []

            print("SYMBOL TEXT IDS BEFORE DISPLAY:", before_text_ids)

            try:
                display_result = attr.DisplayAttribute()
                print("DISPLAY ATTRIBUTE RESULT:", display_result)
            except Exception as e:
                print("DISPLAY ATTRIBUTE ERROR:", block_symbol_id, equipment_attribute_id, repr(e))
                continue

            time.sleep(0.5)

            try:
                after_text_result = sym.GetTextIds(None)
                after_text_ids = extract_ids(after_text_result)
            except Exception as e:
                print("FAILED TO GET SYMBOL TEXT IDS AFTER DISPLAY:", block_symbol_id, repr(e))
                after_text_ids = []

            print("SYMBOL TEXT IDS AFTER DISPLAY:", after_text_ids)

            new_text_ids = [text_id for text_id in after_text_ids if text_id not in before_text_ids]
            matching_text_ids = []

            for symbol_text_id in after_text_ids:
                text.SetId(symbol_text_id)

                try:
                    symbol_text_value = str(text.GetText()).strip()
                except Exception:
                    continue

                if equipment_value and symbol_text_value == equipment_value:
                    matching_text_ids.append(symbol_text_id)

            candidate_text_ids = []

            for text_id in matching_text_ids:
                if text_id not in candidate_text_ids:
                    candidate_text_ids.append(text_id)

            if not candidate_text_ids:
                for text_id in new_text_ids:
                    if text_id not in candidate_text_ids:
                        candidate_text_ids.append(text_id)

            print("NEW SYMBOL TEXT IDS:", new_text_ids)
            print("MATCHING EQUIPMENT TEXT IDS:", matching_text_ids)
            print("CANDIDATE EQUIPMENT TEXT IDS:", candidate_text_ids)

            if not candidate_text_ids:
                print("ATTRIBUTE EXISTS BUT NO DISPLAYED SYMBOL TEXT WAS FOUND:", equipment_attribute_id)
                continue

            for equipment_text_id in candidate_text_ids:
                text.SetId(equipment_text_id)

                try:
                    equipment_text_value = str(text.GetText()).strip()
                except Exception:
                    equipment_text_value = ""

                if equipment_value and equipment_text_value != equipment_value:
                    print("SKIPPING NON-MATCHING NEW TEXT:", equipment_text_id, repr(equipment_text_value))
                    continue

                text.SetVisibility(1)
                text.SetColour(0)
                text.SetMode(1)
                text.SetAlignment(2)
                text.SetStyle(1)
                text.SetHeight(TEXT_SIZE)
                text.SetSchemaLocation(x_center, equipment_y)

                print(
                    "EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION FORMATTED:",
                    "BLOCK:",
                    block_name,
                    "SYMBOL:",
                    block_symbol_id,
                    "ATTR:",
                    equipment_attribute_id,
                    "TEXT:",
                    equipment_text_id,
                    "VALUE:",
                    repr(equipment_text_value),
                    "X:",
                    x_center,
                    "Y:",
                    equipment_y
                )

                formatted_count += 1

        print("")
        print("EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION BLOCKS READ:", block_count)
        print("EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION ATTRIBUTES FOUND:", found_attribute_count)
        print("EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION TEXTS FORMATTED:", formatted_count)

        return formatted_count


    # ================================================
    # SECONDARY RUN
    #
    # After Equipment Identifacation attributes have
    # been turned on, secondary=True means ONLY format
    # those Equipment Identifacation texts and exit.
    #
    # This skips:
    # - connector scanning
    # - pin scanning
    # - component code formatting
    # - device designation formatting
    # - local signal formatting
    # - pin number formatting
    # - 5 second wait
    # - visibility verification
    # ================================================
    if secondary:
        print("")
        print("============================================================")
        print("SECONDARY FORMATTER RUN")
        print("EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION ONLY")
        print("============================================================")

        formatted_count = format_equipment_identification(TEXT_SIZE)

        print("")
        print("============================================================")
        print("SECONDARY FORMATTER COMPLETE")
        print("EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION TEXTS FORMATTED:", formatted_count)
        print("============================================================")

        return formatted_count


    # ================================================
    # TRACK EVERYTHING WE MODIFY
    # ================================================
    modified_texts = []


    print("")
    print("============================================================")
    print("E3 VISIBILITY TEST")
    print("============================================================")


    # ================================================
    # GET ALL LOGICAL DEVICES
    # ================================================
    device_result = prj.GetAllDeviceIds(None)
    device_ids = extract_ids(device_result)


    for device_id in device_ids:
        dev.SetId(device_id)

        try:
            device_name = str(dev.GetName())
        except Exception:
            continue

        # ================================================
        # DETECT CONNECTOR DEVICE
        # ================================================
        try:
            symbol_result = dev.GetSymbolIds(None)
            logical_symbol_ids = extract_ids(symbol_result)
        except Exception:
            logical_symbol_ids = []

        is_connector = False

        for logical_symbol_id in logical_symbol_ids:
            sym.SetId(logical_symbol_id)

            try:
                text_result = sym.GetTextIds(None)
                text_ids = extract_ids(text_result)
            except Exception:
                text_ids = []

            for text_id in text_ids:
                text.SetId(text_id)

                try:
                    text_type = text.GetType()
                except Exception:
                    continue

                if text_type == 5:
                    is_connector = True
                    break

            if is_connector:
                break

        if not is_connector:
            continue

        print("")
        print("############################################################")
        print("CONNECTOR:", device_name)
        print("DEVICE ID:", device_id)
        print("############################################################")

        # ================================================
        # GET LOGICAL CONNECTOR PINS
        # ================================================
        try:
            pin_result = dev.GetPinIds(None)
            logical_pin_ids = extract_ids(pin_result)
        except Exception:
            logical_pin_ids = []

        for logical_pin_id in logical_pin_ids:
            pin.SetId(logical_pin_id)

            try:
                pin_name = str(pin.GetName())
            except Exception:
                pin_name = "UNKNOWN"

            pin_view_ids = get_pin_view_ids(logical_pin_id)

            for pin_view_id in pin_view_ids:
                sym.SetId(pin_view_id)
                sym.SetBlockHatchColour(58)

                try:
                    text_result = sym.GetTextIds(None)
                    symbol_text_ids = extract_ids(text_result)
                except Exception:
                    symbol_text_ids = []

                # ================================================
                # DETERMINE POSITION FROM TYPE 213
                # ================================================
                split_position = None

                for text_id in symbol_text_ids:
                    text.SetId(text_id)

                    try:
                        text_type = text.GetType()
                        text_value = str(text.GetText()).strip()
                    except Exception:
                        continue

                    if text_type == 213 and "/" in text_value and not text_value.startswith("*"):
                        try:
                            split_position = int(text_value.split("/")[0])
                        except Exception:
                            split_position = None

                        break

                if split_position is None:
                    continue

                print("")
                print("PIN:", pin_name, "VIEW:", pin_view_id, "POSITION:", split_position)

                # ================================================
                # TEST EVERY RELEVANT TEXT
                # ================================================
                for text_id in symbol_text_ids:
                    text.SetId(text_id)

                    try:
                        text_type = text.GetType()
                        text_value = str(text.GetText()).strip()
                    except Exception:
                        continue

                    # ================================================
                    # TYPE 5 - COMPONENT CODE
                    # ALWAYS OFF
                    # ================================================
                    if text_type == 5:
                        before = get_visibility(text_id)
                        text.SetId(text_id)
                        result = text.SetVisibility(0)
                        after = get_visibility(text_id)

                        print("    TYPE 5")
                        print("    TEXT ID:", text_id)
                        print("    VALUE:", repr(text_value))
                        print("    BEFORE:", before)
                        print("    SET RESULT:", result)
                        print("    AFTER:", after)

                        if after == 0:
                            print("    IMMEDIATE RESULT: PASS")
                        else:
                            print("    IMMEDIATE RESULT: FAIL")

                        modified_texts.append({
                            "device": device_name,
                            "pin": pin_name,
                            "position": split_position,
                            "view": pin_view_id,
                            "text_id": text_id,
                            "type": 5,
                            "value": text_value,
                            "desired": 0
                        })

                    # ================================================
                    # TYPE 12 - DEVICE DESIGNATION
                    # ONLY TURN OFF NON-TOP PINS
                    # ================================================
                    elif text_type == 12 and split_position != 1:
                        before = get_visibility(text_id)
                        text.SetId(text_id)
                        result = text.SetVisibility(0)
                        after = get_visibility(text_id)

                        print("    TYPE 12 NON-TOP")
                        print("    TEXT ID:", text_id)
                        print("    VALUE:", repr(text_value))
                        print("    BEFORE:", before)
                        print("    SET RESULT:", result)
                        print("    AFTER:", after)

                        if after == 0:
                            print("    IMMEDIATE RESULT: PASS")
                        else:
                            print("    IMMEDIATE RESULT: FAIL")

                        modified_texts.append({
                            "device": device_name,
                            "pin": pin_name,
                            "position": split_position,
                            "view": pin_view_id,
                            "text_id": text_id,
                            "type": 12,
                            "value": text_value,
                            "desired": 0
                        })

                    # ================================================
                    # TYPE 12 TOP PIN
                    # DO NOT TOUCH
                    # ================================================
                    elif text_type == 12 and split_position == 1:
                        visibility = get_visibility(text_id)
                        text.SetStyle(1)
                        text.SetHeight(0.2)

                        print("    TYPE 12 TOP - LEFT ALONE")
                        print("    TEXT ID:", text_id)
                        print("    VALUE:", repr(text_value))
                        print("    VISIBILITY:", visibility)

                    # ================================================
                    # TYPE 617 - LOCAL SIGNAL NAME
                    # ALWAYS ON
                    # ================================================
                    elif text_type == 617:
                        before = get_visibility(text_id)

                        text.SetId(text_id)
                        result = text.SetVisibility(1)
                        text.SetColour(0)
                        text.SetAlignment(3)
                        text.SetStyle(1)
                        text.SetHeight(0.15)

                        after = get_visibility(text_id)

                        print("    TYPE 617 LOCAL SIGNAL")
                        print("    TEXT ID:", text_id)
                        print("    VALUE:", repr(text_value))
                        print("    BEFORE:", before)
                        print("    SET RESULT:", result)
                        print("    AFTER:", after)

                        if after == 1:
                            print("    IMMEDIATE RESULT: PASS")
                        else:
                            print("    IMMEDIATE RESULT: FAIL")

                        modified_texts.append({
                            "device": device_name,
                            "pin": pin_name,
                            "position": split_position,
                            "view": pin_view_id,
                            "text_id": text_id,
                            "type": 617,
                            "value": text_value,
                            "desired": 1
                        })

                    # ================================================
                    # TYPE 203 - PIN NUMBER
                    # ALWAYS VISIBLE AND BOLD
                    # ================================================
                    elif text_type == 203:
                        before = get_visibility(text_id)

                        text.SetId(text_id)
                        result = text.SetVisibility(1)
                        text.SetStyle(1)

                        after = get_visibility(text_id)

                        print("    TYPE 203 PIN NUMBER")
                        print("    TEXT ID:", text_id)
                        print("    VALUE:", repr(text_value))
                        print("    BEFORE:", before)
                        print("    SET RESULT:", result)
                        print("    AFTER:", after)

                        if after == 1:
                            print("    IMMEDIATE RESULT: PASS")
                        else:
                            print("    IMMEDIATE RESULT: FAIL")

                        modified_texts.append({
                            "device": device_name,
                            "pin": pin_name,
                            "position": split_position,
                            "view": pin_view_id,
                            "text_id": text_id,
                            "type": 203,
                            "value": text_value,
                            "desired": 1
                        })


    # ================================================
    # FORMAT THE ACTUAL CURRENT SECONDARY PIN VIEW SYMBOLS
    # ================================================
    # Placing later split pins can replace earlier E3 symbol objects.
    # The IDs returned by PlacePins and saved in the metadata can be stale.
    # Resolve current pin views like the working Block Pin Arranger, then
    # apply formatting to displayed text objects with immediate readback.
    format_current_secondary_pin_views(
        prj, os.environ.get("E3_SECONDARY_BLOCK_FILE"), modified_texts
    )

    # Format top-level FULL MIL connectors only after E3 has placed all views.
    # main.py may have enabled their component code; hide it here.
    format_current_main_connector_symbols(
        prj, os.environ.get("E3_SECONDARY_BLOCK_FILE"),
        modified_texts, 0.2
    )

    # ================================================
    # FORMAT EQUIPMENT IDENTIFACATION ON SECONDARY BLOCKS
    # ================================================
    format_equipment_identification(TEXT_SIZE)


    # ================================================
    # WAIT TO SEE WHETHER E3 RESETS VISIBILITY
    # ================================================
    print("")
    print("============================================================")
    print("IMMEDIATE FORMATTING COMPLETE")
    print("WAITING 1 SECONDS")
    print("============================================================")

    time.sleep(1.0)


    # ================================================
    # SECOND PASS - DO NOT MODIFY ANYTHING
    # JUST READ VISIBILITY BACK
    # ================================================
    print("")
    print("============================================================")
    print("5 SECOND VISIBILITY VERIFICATION")
    print("============================================================")

    passed = 0
    failed = 0

    for item in modified_texts:
        current_visibility = get_visibility(item["text_id"])

        print("")
        print("DEVICE:", item["device"])
        print("PIN:", item["pin"])
        print("POSITION:", item["position"])
        print("VIEW:", item["view"])
        print("TEXT ID:", item["text_id"])
        print("TYPE:", item["type"])
        print("VALUE:", repr(item["value"]))
        print("EXPECTED VISIBILITY:", item["desired"])
        print("ACTUAL VISIBILITY:", current_visibility)

        if current_visibility == item["desired"]:
            print("FINAL RESULT: PASS")
            passed += 1
        else:
            print("FINAL RESULT: FAIL")
            failed += 1


    # ================================================
    # FINAL SUMMARY
    # ================================================
    print("")
    print("============================================================")
    print("FINAL SUMMARY")
    print("============================================================")
    print("PASS:", passed)
    print("FAIL:", failed)
    print("TOTAL:", len(modified_texts))
    print("============================================================")
''',
    'block_maker/error_popup.py': r'''"""Friendly, copyable error popups for the E3 Block Maker launcher.

Standard-library only.  This is imported by the *parent* launcher, so a
failure in a separate main.py / test.py process still produces one popup.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys


def _failure_section(output: str) -> str:
    """Prefer the last Python traceback instead of earlier progress messages."""
    index = output.rfind("Traceback (most recent call last):")
    if index >= 0:
        return output[index:]
    return output[-12000:]


def _exception_line(output: str) -> str:
    lines = [line.strip() for line in _failure_section(output).splitlines() if line.strip()]
    pattern = re.compile(
        r"^(?:[\w.]+(?:Error|Exception)|Exception|SystemExit|KeyboardInterrupt)\s*:"
    )
    for line in reversed(lines):
        if pattern.match(line):
            return line
    # The final line usually holds the COM error or process failure.
    return lines[-1] if lines else "The program stopped without a Python traceback."


def describe_error(stage: str, output: str):
    """Return (title, what_happened, how_to_fix, technical_reason)."""
    relevant = _failure_section(output)
    lower = relevant.lower()
    reason = _exception_line(output)

    checks = (
        ("model check is failing", "Model check failed", "The Excel model check did not pass.", "Open the workbook and correct the model check shown in cell G8. Recalculate/save it, then rerun Block Maker."),
        ("title check is failing", "Title check failed", "The Excel title check did not pass.", "Correct the title check shown in cell E2, recalculate/save the workbook, and rerun."),
        ("connector model check is failing", "Connector model check failed", "One or more connector models failed workbook validation.", "Check the connector model verification at cell G2. Fix the highlighted connector/type entries and rerun."),
        ("duplicate pin check is failing", "Duplicate pins detected", "The workbook detected a duplicate pin assignment.", "Review the duplicate-pin check in cell G4 and correct repeated pin entries before rerunning."),
    )
    for needle, title, happened, fix in checks:
        if needle in lower:
            return title, happened, fix, reason

    if "e3_secondary_block_file" in lower or "metadata not found" in lower or "metadata file not found" in lower:
        return (
            "Launcher / metadata issue",
            "The builder or formatter could not find the temporary data file that connects the two stages.",
            "Launch launcher.py, not main.py or test.py. Keep launcher.py, main.py, test.py, utils.py and error_popup.py together. If packaging an EXE, rebuild it with the updated launcher.",
            reason,
        )
    if "nameerror" in lower and "text_size" in lower:
        return (
            "Text size setting missing",
            "test.py attempted to use TEXT_SIZE without loading it from the builder's metadata.",
            "Ensure main.py saves 'text_size' into each secondary-block metadata entry and test.py reads it before calling runn().",
            reason,
        )
    if "no device found in database" in lower or "step 1 failed" in lower or "database table selected components" in lower:
        return (
            "Connector model not found",
            "E3 could not find a requested connector component in its component database.",
            "Check the connector type and library version (currently 1) in the workbook. Make sure the component exists in the connected E3 library.",
            reason,
        )
    if "pin " in lower and "not found in new view" in lower or "duplicate pin name" in lower:
        return (
            "Schematic pin mismatch",
            "A pin from Excel could not be matched uniquely to the newly created E3 connector view.",
            "Compare the Excel pin names with the pin names in the E3 connector library. Check for missing, differently suffixed, or duplicate pins.",
            reason,
        )
    if "place block failed" in lower:
        return (
            "Block failed to place",
            "Python tried to place a view of a block but failed to do so.",
            "Make sure that there is nothing else on the page, if there is it could have tried to place on top of that.",
            reason,
        )
    if any(x in lower for x in (
        "failed placing split block", "failed creating shared main schematic view", "failed creating new view from parent connector",
        "e3 reused symbol id", "e3 reused pin symbol", "cannot place", "place block failed",
        "failed to create new sheet", "schematic block for",
    )):
        return (
            "E3 schematic placement failed",
            "E3 could not create or place one of the schematic blocks, connector views, pins or sheets.",
            "Inspect the named connector, sheet and symbol in Technical Details. Check available page space and symbol-library names. Because E3 changes already made are not automatically undone, inspect/restore the project before retrying.",
            reason,
        )
    if "ct.application" in lower or "win32com" in lower or "pywintypes.com_error" in lower or "com_error" in lower or "operation unavailable" in lower:
        return (
            "E3 connection or automation error",
            "The Block Maker could not communicate reliably with the active Zuken E3 application.",
            "Open E3 with the correct project, confirm that no modal dialogs are blocking it, and retry. If it persists, copy the technical details so the failing E3 API call can be identified.",
            reason,
        )
    if "pyodbc" in lower or "sql server" in lower or "sql-zuken-prd" in lower or "odbc" in lower:
        return (
            "SQL database connection failed",
            "Block Maker could not access the Zuken SQL database or execute a database request.",
            "Check your corporate network/VPN, SQL Server availability and Windows database permissions. Copy the technical details for the exact database message.",
            reason,
        )
    if "permissionerror" in lower or "permission denied" in lower:
        return (
            "File access denied",
            "Python was not allowed to open or write a required file.",
            "Close any other program holding the file, verify write permissions for the selected folder, and rerun.",
            reason,
        )
    if "filenotfounderror" in lower or "no such file or directory" in lower:
        return (
            "Required file not found",
            "A selected workbook or supporting file could not be found.",
            "Verify the workbook path and that all Block Maker scripts are in the same folder. Then rerun from launcher.py.",
            reason,
        )
    if any(x in lower for x in ("badzipfile", "invalidfileexception", "openpyxl", "load_workbook", "not a zip file")):
        return (
            "Excel workbook could not be read",
            "Block Maker could not read the selected Excel workbook correctly.",
            "Select a valid .xlsm workbook based on the Block Maker template; verify it is saved and not corrupted. Check the workbook values required by main.py.",
            reason,
        )
    if "keyerror" in lower or "nonetype" in lower or "typeerror" in lower or "valueerror" in lower:
        return (
            "Missing or invalid input data",
            "A required Excel value or connector field was missing or in an unexpected format.",
            "Review the connector list, pin counts, names, and required workbook cells. Technical Details identifies the exact line and value that failed.",
            reason,
        )
    if stage.lower().startswith("formatter"):
        return (
            "Formatter stopped",
            "The E3 schematic builder completed, but the formatting stage did not finish.",
            "Review Technical Details for the failing symbol or attribute. The E3 project may contain completed blocks even though formatting failed; avoid rerunning the builder on top of them without inspecting the project.",
            reason,
        )
    return (
        "Block Maker stopped",
        f"The {stage.lower()} stage failed before the entire operation finished.",
        "Read Technical Details and use Copy Error to share the complete error and program output. Check the E3 project for partially created objects before rerunning.",
        reason,
    )


def show_error_dialog(stage: str, output: str, log_path: str, exit_code: int) -> None:
    """Show friendly popup. Copy Error includes the *entire* logged run."""
    title, happened, fix, reason = describe_error(stage, output)
    path = Path(log_path)
    try:
        full_log = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:
        full_log = output

    copy_payload = (
        "E3 BLOCK MAKER — ERROR REPORT\n"
        f"Stage: {stage}\n"
        f"Issue: {title}\n"
        f"What happened: {happened}\n"
        f"How to fix it: {fix}\n"
        f"Technical error: {reason}\n"
        f"Exit code: {exit_code}\n"
        f"Log file: {path}\n"
        "\n=========== FULL PROGRAM OUTPUT ===========\n"
        f"{full_log}"
    )
    technical_preview = full_log[-24000:]  # Keep popup responsive for very long logs.

    try:
        import tkinter as tk
        from tkinter import scrolledtext, ttk

        root = tk.Tk()
        root.title("Block Maker - Error")
        root.configure(bg="#f5f7fa")
        root.geometry("800x390")
        root.minsize(670, 340)
        root.attributes("-topmost", True)

        outer = tk.Frame(root, bg="#f5f7fa", padx=22, pady=16)
        outer.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        heading = tk.Label(outer, text="Block Maker could not finish", fg="#a52730", bg="#f5f7fa",
                           font=("Segoe UI", 16, "bold"), anchor="w")
        heading.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 6))

        issue = tk.Label(outer, text=f"{title}  |  {stage}", fg="#263238", bg="#f5f7fa",
                         font=("Segoe UI", 11, "bold"), anchor="w")
        issue.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))

        tk.Label(outer, text="WHAT HAPPENED", fg="#526577", bg="#f5f7fa",
                 font=("Segoe UI", 9, "bold"), anchor="w").pack(fill="x")
        happened_label = tk.Label(outer, text=happened, fg="#233142", bg="#f5f7fa",
                                  font=("Segoe UI", 10), justify="left", anchor="w", wraplength=745)
        happened_label.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 10))

        tk.Label(outer, text="HOW TO FIX IT", fg="#526577", bg="#f5f7fa",
                 font=("Segoe UI", 9, "bold"), anchor="w").pack(fill="x")
        fix_label = tk.Label(outer, text=fix, fg="#233142", bg="#f5f7fa",
                             font=("Segoe UI", 10), justify="left", anchor="w", wraplength=745)
        fix_label.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 10))

        reason_label = tk.Label(outer, text=f"Error: {reason}", fg="#526577", bg="#f5f7fa",
                                font=("Consolas", 9), justify="left", anchor="w", wraplength=745)
        reason_label.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 6))

        log_label = tk.Label(outer, text=f"Log saved: {path}", fg="#526577", bg="#f5f7fa",
                              font=("Segoe UI", 9), anchor="w", wraplength=745, justify="left")
        log_label.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 8))

        details_frame = tk.Frame(outer, bg="#f5f7fa")
        details_box = scrolledtext.ScrolledText(details_frame, height=12, wrap="none",
                                               font=("Consolas", 9), relief="solid", bd=1)
        details_box.insert("1.0", technical_preview)
        details_box.configure(state="disabled")
        details_box.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        status = tk.StringVar(value="")
        status_label = tk.Label(outer, textvariable=status, fg="#236d3f", bg="#f5f7fa",
                                font=("Segoe UI", 9), anchor="w")
        status_label.pack(side="bottom", fill="x", pady=(5, 0))

        button_bar = tk.Frame(outer, bg="#f5f7fa")
        button_bar.pack(side="bottom", fill="x", pady=(6, 2))

        details_visible = [False]

        def toggle_details():
            details_visible[0] = not details_visible[0]
            if details_visible[0]:
                details_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True, before=button_bar, pady=(4, 6))
                details_button.configure(text="Hide Technical Details")
                root.geometry("800x675")
            else:
                details_frame.pack_forget()
                details_button.configure(text="Show Technical Details")
                root.geometry("800x390")

        def copy_error():
            try:
                root.clipboard_clear()
                root.clipboard_append(copy_payload)
                root.update()  # Keep clipboard data available after clicking.
                status.set("Full error report and program output copied to clipboard.")
            except Exception as exc:
                status.set(f"Couldn't copy automatically: {exc}. Log is saved above.")

        details_button = ttk.Button(button_bar, text="Show Technical Details", command=toggle_details)
        details_button.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 9))
        ttk.Button(button_bar, text="Copy Error", command=copy_error).pack(side="left")
        ttk.Button(button_bar, text="Close", command=root.destroy).pack(side="right")

        root.lift()
        root.focus_force()
        root.mainloop()
    except Exception as popup_exc:
        # Never hide the original exception if Tk cannot open a window.
        sys.stderr.write(f"Error popup unavailable: {popup_exc!r}\n{copy_payload}\n")

def log(message=""):

def is_missing_value(value):
    if value is None:
        return True
    try:
        return int(value) == COM_MISSING
    except Exception:
        return False
def clean_id_list(values):
    cleaned = []
    if values is None:
        return cleaned
    if not isinstance(values, (tuple, list)):
        values = [values]
    for value in values:
        if is_missing_value(value):
            continue
        try:
            value = int(value)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if value > 0:
            cleaned.append(value)
    return cleaned
def parse_count_array_result(result):
    if result is None:
        return []
    if isinstance(result, (tuple, list)):
        items = list(result)
        if len(items) >= 2 and isinstance(items[1], (tuple, list)):
            try:
                count = int(items[0])
            except Exception:
                count = None
            values = clean_id_list(items[1])
            if count is not None and len(values) > count:
                values = values[-count:]
            return values
        return clean_id_list(items)
    return clean_id_list(result)
def call_id_array(method):
    try:
        result = method(None)
        values = parse_count_array_result(result)
        if values:
            return values
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        result = method()
        values = parse_count_array_result(result)
        if values:
            return values
    except Exception:
        pass
    return []
def call_noarg(obj, method_name):
    if not hasattr(obj, method_name):
        return None
    try:
        return getattr(obj, method_name)()
    except Exception:
        return None
def normalize_pin_value(value):
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, int):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, float):
        if math.isfinite(value) and value.is_integer():
            return str(int(value))
        return str(value).strip()
    return str(value).strip()
def median(values):
    if not values:
        return None
    ordered = sorted(values)
    middle = len(ordered) // 2
    if len(ordered) % 2 == 0:
        return (ordered[middle - 1] + ordered[middle]) / 2.0
    return ordered[middle]
def get_device_ids(prj):
    return call_id_array(prj.GetAllDeviceIds)
def get_pin_ids(dev):
    return call_id_array(dev.GetPinIds)
def get_view_ids(pin):
    return call_id_array(pin.GetViewIds)
def get_node_ids(pin):

def get_bound_ids(sym):

def get_text_ids(sym):

            device_name = str(dev.GetName()).strip()

def get_symbol_schema_location(sym):
    try:
        result = sym.GetSchemaLocation(None, None, None, None, None)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if not isinstance(result, (tuple, list)):
        return None
    if len(result) < 3:
        return None
    try:
        sheet_id = int(result[0])
        x = float(result[1])
        y = float(result[2])
    except Exception:
        return None
    return {
        "sheet_id": sheet_id,
        "x": x,
        "y": y,
        "raw": result,
    }
def get_symbol_placed_area(sym):
    try:
        result = sym.GetPlacedArea(None, None, None, None)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if not isinstance(result, (tuple, list)):
        return None
    if len(result) < 5:
        return None
    try:
        success = int(result[0])
        left = float(result[1])
        bottom = float(result[2])
        right = float(result[3])
        top = float(result[4])
    except Exception:
        return None
    if success <= 0:
        return None
    return {
        "left": left,
        "bottom": bottom,
        "right": right,
        "top": top,
        "width": right - left,
        "height": top - bottom,
        "center_x": (left + right) / 2.0,
        "center_y": (bottom + top) / 2.0,
        "raw": result,
    }
def get_pin_schema_location(pin, pin_view_id):
    pin.SetId(pin_view_id)
    try:
        result = pin.GetSchemaLocation(None, None, None, None, None)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if not isinstance(result, (tuple, list)):
        return None
    numeric_values = []
    for value in result:
        if is_missing_value(value):
            continue
        try:
            numeric_values.append(float(value))
        except Exception:
            pass
    if len(numeric_values) < 3:
        return None
    return numeric_values
def resolve_live_symbol_from_pin_view(prj, pin_view_id):
    local_sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
    try:
        live_symbol_id = local_sym.SetId(pin_view_id)
    except Exception:
        live_symbol_id = 0
    try:
        live_symbol_id = int(live_symbol_id)
    except Exception:
        live_symbol_id = 0
    if live_symbol_id <= 0:
        return None
    return {
        "symbol_object": local_sym,
        "live_symbol_id": live_symbol_id,
    }
def inspect_live_symbol_by_id(prj, symbol_id):
    local_sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
    try:
        set_result = local_sym.SetId(symbol_id)
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        set_result = int(set_result)
    except Exception:
        set_result = 0
    if set_result <= 0:
        return None
    return {
        "symbol_id": call_noarg(local_sym, "GetId"),
        "symbol_type_name": call_noarg(local_sym, "GetSymbolTypeName"),
        "characteristic": call_noarg(local_sym, "GetCharacteristic"),
        "rotation": call_noarg(local_sym, "GetRotation"),
        "scaling": call_noarg(local_sym, "GetScaling"),
        "schema_location": get_symbol_schema_location(local_sym),
        "bound_ids": get_bound_ids(local_sym),
        "text_ids": get_text_ids(local_sym),
    }
def location_is_inside_block(location, block_geometry, tolerance=0.10):
    if location is None:
        return False
    try:
        x = float(location["x"])
        y = float(location["y"])
        sheet_id = int(location["sheet_id"])
    except Exception:
        return False
    if sheet_id != int(block_geometry["sheet_id"]):
        return False
    if x < block_geometry["block_left"] - tolerance:
        return False
    if x > block_geometry["block_right"] + tolerance:
        return False
    if y < block_geometry["block_bottom"] - tolerance:
        return False
    if y > block_geometry["block_top"] + tolerance:
        return False
    return True
def discover_current_connector_pin_symbols(prj, sht, connector_name, block_geometry):
    candidate_ids = collect_live_symbol_candidates(prj, sht, block_geometry["sheet_id"])
    discovered = {}
    for candidate_id in candidate_ids:
        identity = read_symbol_identity(prj, candidate_id)
        if identity is None:
            continue
        if (identity["location"] is None or
                identity["location"]["sheet_id"] != block_geometry["sheet_id"]):
            continue
        pin_number = identity["pin_number"]
        device_text = identity["device_text"]
        if pin_number in (None, ""):
            continue
        if not isinstance(device_text, str):
            continue
        if not match_connector_designation(device_text, connector_name):
            continue
        if not location_is_inside_block(identity["location"], block_geometry):
            continue
        metadata = inspect_live_symbol_by_id(prj, identity["symbol_id"])
        if metadata is None:
            continue
        candidate = {
            "symbol_id": identity["symbol_id"],
            "location": identity["location"],
            "symbol_type_name": identity["symbol_type_name"],
            "characteristic": identity["characteristic"],
            "rotation": metadata["rotation"],
            "scaling": metadata["scaling"],
            "bound_ids": metadata["bound_ids"],
        }
        if pin_number not in discovered:
            discovered[pin_number] = candidate
            continue
        old = discovered[pin_number]
        old_type = old["symbol_type_name"]
        new_type = candidate["symbol_type_name"]
        preferred_types = {
            "MilSymbolReceptacle¶END_TOP",
            "MilSymbolReceptacle¶END_SPLIT",
            "MilSymbolReceptacle¶SPLIT_BOTTOM",
            "MilSymbolReceptacle¶CONNECTOR_PIN",
            "MilSymbolReceptacle¶END_BOTTOM",
        }
        if new_type in preferred_types and old_type not in preferred_types:
            discovered[pin_number] = candidate
    return discovered
def resolve_current_live_pin_symbol(prj, sht, connector_name, pin_name, block_geometry):
    discovered = discover_current_connector_pin_symbols(prj, sht, connector_name, block_geometry)
    return discovered.get(normalize_pin_value(pin_name))
def snapshot_connector(prj, dev, pin, sht, connector_device_id, connector_name, block_geometry):
    snapshot = []
    discovered_live_symbols = discover_current_connector_pin_symbols(prj, sht, connector_name, block_geometry)
    dev.SetId(connector_device_id)
    for logical_pin_id in get_pin_ids(dev):
        pin.SetId(logical_pin_id)
        pin_name = normalize_pin_value(pin.GetName())
        pin_view_ids = get_view_ids(pin)
        record = {
            "pin_name": pin_name,
            "logical_pin_id": logical_pin_id,
            "view_ids": pin_view_ids,
            "views": [],
        }
        for pin_view_id in pin_view_ids:
            location = get_pin_schema_location(pin, pin_view_id)
            node_ids = get_node_ids(pin)
            live = resolve_live_symbol_from_pin_view(prj, pin_view_id)
            view_record = {
                "pin_view_id": pin_view_id,
                "location": location,
                "node_ids": node_ids,
                "live_symbol_id": None,
                "symbol_type_name": None,
                "characteristic": None,
                "rotation": None,
                "scaling": None,
                "bound_ids": [],
                "resolution_source": None,
            }
            if live is not None:
                live_sym = live["symbol_object"]
                view_record["live_symbol_id"] = live["live_symbol_id"]
                view_record["symbol_type_name"] = call_noarg(live_sym, "GetSymbolTypeName")
                view_record["characteristic"] = call_noarg(live_sym, "GetCharacteristic")
                view_record["rotation"] = call_noarg(live_sym, "GetRotation")
                view_record["scaling"] = call_noarg(live_sym, "GetScaling")
                view_record["bound_ids"] = get_bound_ids(live_sym)
                view_record["resolution_source"] = "stable pin-view ID"
                live_location = get_symbol_schema_location(live_sym)
                if live_location is not None:
                    view_record["location"] = [
                        live_location["sheet_id"],
                        live_location["x"],
                        live_location["y"],
                    ]
            else:
                discovered = discovered_live_symbols.get(pin_name)
                if discovered is not None:
                    view_record["live_symbol_id"] = discovered["symbol_id"]
                    view_record["symbol_type_name"] = discovered["symbol_type_name"]
                    view_record["characteristic"] = discovered["characteristic"]
                    view_record["rotation"] = discovered["rotation"]
                    view_record["scaling"] = discovered["scaling"]
                    view_record["bound_ids"] = discovered["bound_ids"]
                    view_record["resolution_source"] = "sheet scan by connector + pin + block"
                    discovered_location = discovered["location"]
                    if discovered_location is not None:
                        view_record["location"] = [
                            discovered_location["sheet_id"],
                            discovered_location["x"],
                            discovered_location["y"],
                        ]
            record["views"].append(view_record)
        snapshot.append(record)
    return snapshot
def get_sorted_live_pin_records(snapshot):
    records = []
    for pin_record in snapshot:
        if len(pin_record["views"]) != 1:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Pin {pin_record['pin_name']} does not have exactly one pin view.")
        view = pin_record["views"][0]
        if view["live_symbol_id"] is None:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Pin {pin_record['pin_name']} does not have a live symbol. Stable view ID and connector/block sheet scan both failed.")
        location = view["location"]
        if location is None or len(location) < 3:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Pin {pin_record['pin_name']} does not have a valid schema location.")
        records.append({
            "pin_name": pin_record["pin_name"],
            "logical_pin_id": pin_record["logical_pin_id"],
            "pin_view_id": view["pin_view_id"],
            "live_symbol_id": view["live_symbol_id"],
            "sheet_id": int(location[0]),
            "x": float(location[1]),
            "y": float(location[2]),
            "symbol_type_name": view["symbol_type_name"],
            "characteristic": view["characteristic"],
            "rotation": view["rotation"],
            "scaling": view["scaling"],
        })
    records.sort(key=lambda item: item["y"], reverse=True)
    return records
def normalize_optional_text(value):
    """Blank Excel cells mean no restriction; numbers like 2.0 mean '2'."""
    value = normalize_pin_value(value)
    return value if value not in (None, "") else None


def parse_optional_vertical_spacing(value):
    """Excel J1: optional center-to-center Y spacing in E3 schematic units.

    Empty J1 preserves automatic pitch detection and all existing positioning.
    Reject malformed/zero/negative values before any E3 pin modification.
    """
    if value is None or (isinstance(value, str) and not value.strip()):
        return None
    try:
        spacing = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Excel J1 vertical pin spacing must be a positive number; got {value!r}."
        ) from exc
    if not math.isfinite(spacing) or spacing <= 0:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Excel J1 vertical pin spacing must be finite and greater than zero; got {value!r}."
        )
    return spacing


def find_device_ids(prj, dev, target_name, all_device_ids=None):
    """Unlike find_device_id, keep duplicates so they cannot be selected silently."""
    ids = all_device_ids if all_device_ids is not None else get_device_ids(prj)
    matches = []
    for device_id in ids:
        try:
            dev.SetId(device_id)
            name = str(dev.GetName()).strip()
        except Exception:
            continue
        if name == target_name:
            matches.append(device_id)
    return matches


def get_sheet_metadata(prj, sht):
    """Resolve user-facing sheet names/numbers to E3's internal sheet IDs."""
    sheets = []
    for sheet_id in call_id_array(prj.GetAllSheetIds):
        try:
            sht.SetId(sheet_id)
            name = str(sht.GetName()).strip()
        except Exception:
            continue
        aliases = [name]
        # The installed E3 API version may or may not provide these methods.
        for method_name in ("GetSheetNumber", "GetNumber"):
            raw = call_noarg(sht, method_name)
            alias = normalize_optional_text(raw)
            if alias is not None:
                aliases.append(alias)
        sheets.append({"sheet_id": sheet_id, "name": name, "aliases": aliases})
    if not sheets:
        raise RuntimeError("No sheets were found in the current E3 project.")
    return sheets


def same_sheet_label(left, right):
    a = str(left).strip().casefold()
    b = str(right).strip().casefold()
    if a == b:
        return True
    # Only treat whole-number names like '02' and '2' as equivalent.
    try:
        return a.isdecimal() and b.isdecimal() and int(a) == int(b)
    except Exception:
        return False


def resolve_optional_sheet_id(sheet_metadata, requested_page):
    if requested_page is None:
        return None
    matches = [
        sheet for sheet in sheet_metadata
        if any(same_sheet_label(alias, requested_page) for alias in sheet["aliases"])
    ]
    if len(matches) == 1:
        return matches[0]["sheet_id"]
    if not matches:
        available = ", ".join(repr(item["name"]) for item in sheet_metadata)
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Excel J2 page {requested_page!r} does not match an E3 sheet name/number. "
            f"Available E3 sheets: {available}"
        )
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"Excel J2 page {requested_page!r} matches multiple E3 sheets. "
        "Use a unique sheet name/number in J2."
    )


def get_live_connector_pin_locations(prj, dev, pin, connector_device_id):
    """Find where THIS logical connector's live pin views are actually placed.

    Avoid connector text scans for initial disambiguation: a populated E3
    project may reuse both pin numbers and connector names.
    """
    locations = []
    dev.SetId(connector_device_id)
    logical_pin_ids = get_pin_ids(dev)
    for logical_pin_id in logical_pin_ids:
        try:
            pin.SetId(logical_pin_id)
            pin_name = normalize_pin_value(pin.GetName())
            pin_view_ids = get_view_ids(pin)
        except Exception:
            continue
        for pin_view_id in pin_view_ids:
            live = resolve_live_symbol_from_pin_view(prj, pin_view_id)
            if live is None:
                continue
            location = get_symbol_schema_location(live["symbol_object"])
            if location is not None:
                locations.append({"pin_name": pin_name, "location": location})
    return locations


def collect_candidate_cabblocks(prj, dev, main_device_ids, requested_sheet_id=None):
    """Collect EVERY CABBLOCK in matching device views, never the first one."""
    blocks = []
    for main_device_id in main_device_ids:
        for main_view_id in get_device_view_ids(dev, main_device_id):
            main_view_number = get_view_number(dev, main_view_id)
            if main_view_number is None:
                continue
            for symbol_id in get_device_symbol_ids(dev, main_view_id):
                local_sym = prj.CreateSymbolObject()
                try:
                    if not local_sym.SetId(symbol_id):
                        continue
                    symbol_type_name = str(local_sym.GetSymbolTypeName())
                except Exception:
                    continue
                if "CABBLOCK" not in symbol_type_name.upper():
                    continue
                location = get_symbol_schema_location(local_sym)
                if location is None:
                    continue
                sheet_id = location["sheet_id"]
                if requested_sheet_id is not None and sheet_id != requested_sheet_id:
                    continue
                area = get_symbol_placed_area(local_sym)
                if area is None:
                    continue
                blocks.append({
                    "main_device_id": main_device_id,
                    "main_view_id": main_view_id,
                    "connector_view_number": main_view_number,
                    "block_symbol_id": symbol_id,
                    "block_symbol_type": symbol_type_name,
                    "block_schema_location": location,
                    "block_left": area["left"],
                    "block_right": area["right"],
                    "block_bottom": area["bottom"],
                    "block_top": area["top"],
                    "block_width": area["width"],
                    "block_height": area["height"],
                    "block_center_x": area["center_x"],
                    "block_center_y": area["center_y"],
                    "sheet_id": sheet_id,
                })
    return blocks



# ================================================
# READ-ONLY TARGET DEBUGGING (NO E3 CHANGES)
