    ttk.Label(frame, text=f"{result.get('verified', 0)} pin local signal(s) updated").pack()
    ttk.Label(frame, text='Press Enter to close', foreground='#555555').pack(pady=(6, 5))

    def finish(event=None):
        try:
            window.grab_release()
        except tk.TclError:
            pass
        if window.winfo_exists():
            window.destroy()

    window.protocol('WM_DELETE_WINDOW', finish)
    window.bind('<Return>', finish)
    window.bind('<KP_Enter>', finish)
    window.bind('<Escape>', finish)
    close_button = ttk.Button(frame, text='Close', command=finish)
    close_button.pack()

    def bring_to_front():
        if not window.winfo_exists():
            return
        try:
            window.deiconify()
            window.attributes('-topmost', True)
            window.lift()
            window.focus_force()
            close_button.focus_force()
        except tk.TclError:
            return

        # On Windows, visual topmost position does not always equal keyboard
        # focus; explicitly request foreground activation for the dialog.
        if sys.platform == 'win32':
            try:
                import ctypes
                from ctypes import wintypes
                user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
                user32.GetAncestor.argtypes = (wintypes.HWND, wintypes.UINT)
                user32.GetAncestor.restype = wintypes.HWND
                user32.ShowWindow.argtypes = (wintypes.HWND, ctypes.c_int)
                user32.ShowWindow.restype = wintypes.BOOL
                user32.BringWindowToTop.argtypes = (wintypes.HWND,)
                user32.BringWindowToTop.restype = wintypes.BOOL
                user32.SetForegroundWindow.argtypes = (wintypes.HWND,)
                user32.SetForegroundWindow.restype = wintypes.BOOL
                hwnd = user32.GetAncestor(int(window.winfo_id()), 2)  # GA_ROOT
                if not hwnd:
                    hwnd = int(window.winfo_id())
                user32.ShowWindow(hwnd, 5)  # SW_SHOW
                user32.BringWindowToTop(hwnd)
                user32.SetForegroundWindow(hwnd)
                close_button.focus_force()
            except Exception:
                # Tk's topmost/focus_force are the cross-platform fallback.
                pass

    # Make it modal with respect to the importer's hidden Tk root. This also
    # ensures keyboard events go to this window once foregrounded.
    window.update_idletasks()
    try:
        window.grab_set()
    except tk.TclError:
        pass
    bring_to_front()
    # A delayed focus request handles Windows/E3 briefly reclaiming focus.
    window.after(120, bring_to_front)
    window.after(350, bring_to_front)
    parent.wait_window(window)


def show_issue_window(parent, summary, issues, title='Zuken Import — Issues'):
    """Compact selectable scrollable error report, no spawned console or log."""
    import tkinter as tk
    from tkinter import ttk

    window = tk.Toplevel(parent)
    window.title(title + ' (import already finished)')
    window.geometry('700x385')
    window.minsize(460, 250)
    try:
        # Do NOT set transient(parent): the file-picker root is withdrawn,
        # and Windows can hide a transient child of a withdrawn parent.
        window.attributes('-topmost', True)
        window.deiconify()
        window.lift()
    except Exception:
        pass
    frame = ttk.Frame(window, padding=12)
    frame.pack(fill='both', expand=True)
    ttk.Label(frame, text='Import finished — review the items below',
              font=('Segoe UI', 11, 'bold')).pack(anchor='w', pady=(0, 7))
    ttk.Label(frame, text='\n'.join(issues), justify='left',
              wraplength=660).pack(anchor='w', pady=(0, 7))
    ttk.Label(frame, text='Details (select text or click Copy details):').pack(anchor='w')
    body = ttk.Frame(frame)
    body.pack(fill='both', expand=True, pady=(4, 10))
    scrollbar = ttk.Scrollbar(body)
    scrollbar.pack(side='right', fill='y')
    text_box = tk.Text(body, wrap='word', height=9, yscrollcommand=scrollbar.set,
                       font=('Consolas', 9))
    text_box.pack(side='left', fill='both', expand=True)
    scrollbar.config(command=text_box.yview)
    records = summary.get('output_lines') or []
    relevant = []
    tokens = ('BLOCK LOOKUP', 'BLOCK MATCH', 'BLOCK ', 'SKIP ', 'ERROR',
              'FATAL', 'STOP', 'NOTICE:', 'FAILED', 'WARNING', 'SUMMARY',
              'Pin-local attributes written:', 'Pin-local value readback verified:',
              'Pin-local text formatting verified:', 'Top-pin designations bold/visible:',
              'ALL LOCAL SIGNAL LABELS VISIBLE', 'VISIBILITY ERROR',
              'Local signal labels visible', 'Local label visibility')
    for line in records:
        if any(tag in line for tag in tokens):
            relevant.append(line)
    if not relevant:
        relevant = list(records[-50:])
    display = '\n'.join(relevant[-180:])
    full = '\n'.join(records)
    text_box.insert('1.0', display)
    text_box.config(state='disabled')
    row = ttk.Frame(frame)
    row.pack(fill='x')

    def copy_text():
        window.clipboard_clear()
        window.clipboard_append(full)
        window.update()

    ttk.Button(row, text='Copy full diagnostic output', command=copy_text).pack(side='left')
    ttk.Button(row, text='Close', command=window.destroy).pack(side='right')
    window.protocol('WM_DELETE_WINDOW', window.destroy)
    window.lift()
    window.focus_force()
    print('IMPORT FINISHED WITH ISSUES — visible issue window open; click Close to exit.', flush=True)
    parent.wait_window(window)
    print('Issue window closed; script exiting.', flush=True)


def run_once():
    """Pick workbook, import quietly, and show success or actionable errors."""
    import tkinter as tk
    from tkinter import filedialog

    root = tk.Tk()
    root.withdraw()
    try:
        root.attributes('-topmost', True)
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        import os as _mega_os
        filename = _mega_os.environ.get("E3_MEGA_SIGNAL_WORKBOOK", "").strip() or filedialog.askopenfilename(
            parent=root,
            title='Select Excel file for E3 local signal names',
            filetypes=[('Excel workbooks', '*.xlsx *.xlsm'), ('All files', '*.*')],
        )
        if not filename:
            return

        # In a normal E3 run, keep the output pane clean. We retain all existing
        # diagnostics in memory and display them if the import actually fails.
        capture = io.StringIO()
        with redirect_stdout(capture), redirect_stderr(capture), warnings.catch_warnings(record=True) as caught:
            warnings.simplefilter('always')
            result = perform(Path(filename))

        extra = capture.getvalue().splitlines()
        if extra:
            # perform() already saves ConsoleLines into output_lines, so keep
            # captured stdout only as extra diagnostics if an issue occurs.
            result.setdefault('output_lines', []).extend(
                ['--- Additional captured output ---'] + extra[-250:]
            )
        if caught:
            result.setdefault('output_lines', []).extend(
                ['--- Python warnings ---'] + [str(w.message) for w in caught]
            )

        issues = import_issues(result)
        if issues:
            show_issue_window(root, result, issues)
        else:
            show_complete_window(root, result)
    except Exception as exc:
        details = traceback.format_exc()
        try:
            show_issue_window(root,
                {'output_lines': details.splitlines()},
                [f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'],
                title='Zuken Import - Unexpected Error')
        except Exception:
            try:
                from tkinter import messagebox
                messagebox.showerror('Zuken Import - Unexpected Error',
                                     f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}', parent=root)
            except Exception:
                # Keep the exception visible if tkinter's error dialog is unavailable.
                print('UNEXPECTED ERROR:\n' + details, flush=True)
    finally:
        root.destroy()


def main():
    run_once()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
''',
}

def source_digest(source: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(source.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()


def runtime_directory() -> Path:
    """Use local folder when writable, otherwise a per-user Windows folder."""
    preferred = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / 'E3_Mega_Runtime_V13'
    try:
        preferred.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=str(preferred), delete=True):
            pass
        return preferred
    except OSError:
        home = Path(os.environ.get('LOCALAPPDATA') or Path.home())
        fallback = home / 'E3_Mega_Runtime_V13'
        fallback.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return fallback


def prefs_file() -> Path:
    folder = Path(os.environ.get('LOCALAPPDATA') or Path.home()) / 'E3_Mega_Tools'
    folder.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    return folder / 'prefs.json'


def read_prefs() -> dict:
    try:
        data=json.loads(prefs_file().read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        if isinstance(data,dict):
            return {key:str(data.get(key,'')).strip() for key in WORKBOOKS}
    except (OSError,ValueError,TypeError):
        pass
    return {key:'' for key in WORKBOOKS}


def write_prefs(data: dict) -> None:
    dest=prefs_file()
    tmp=dest.with_suffix('.tmp')
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps({k:data.get(k,'') for k in WORKBOOKS}, indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    os.replace(tmp,dest)


def install_one(relative_path: str, base: Path) -> tuple[Path,str]:
    """Materialize original source; keep local edits if any."""
    if relative_path not in SOURCE_FILES:
        raise KeyError(relative_path)
    path=base / relative_path
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    raw=SOURCE_FILES[relative_path].encode('utf-8')
    digest=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
    compile(SOURCE_FILES[relative_path],relative_path,'exec')
    tracker=path.with_name(path.name+'.bundled.sha256')
    if path.exists():
        current=hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        if current==digest:
            if not tracker.exists():tracker.write_text(digest+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
            return path,'unchanged'
        previous=tracker.read_text(encoding='utf-8').strip() if tracker.exists() else ''
        if current!=previous:
            return path,'kept user-edited file'
        backup=path.with_name(path.name+'.backup.'+datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S'))
        shutil.copy2(path,backup)
    tmp=path.with_name(path.name+'.updating')
    try:
        tmp.write_bytes(raw)
        os.replace(tmp,path)
        tracker.write_text(digest+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    finally:
        if tmp.exists():tmp.unlink()
    return path,'installed'


def install_tool(tool_id: str, base: Path) -> tuple[Path,list[str]]:
    tool=TOOLS[tool_id]
    entry=tool['entry']
    folder=entry.split('/')[0]+'/'
    files=[k for k in SOURCE_FILES if k.startswith(folder)]
    actions=[]
    for relative in files:
        _,action=install_one(relative,base)
        actions.append(f'{relative}: {action}')
    return base/entry,actions


def open_folder(folder: Path) -> None:
    folder.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    if sys.platform=='win32':
        os.startfile(str(folder))
    elif sys.platform=='darwin':
        subprocess.Popen(['open',str(folder)])
    else:
        subprocess.Popen(['xdg-open',str(folder)])


class MegaApp:
    def __init__(self):
        self.root=tk.Tk()
        self.root.title('E3 Mega Tools | All-in-One')
        self.root.configure(bg=PALETTE['white'])
        self.root.geometry('608x702+70+70')
        self.root.resizable(False,False)
        self.root.attributes('-topmost',True)
        self.root.protocol('WM_DELETE_WINDOW',self.close)
        self.runtime=runtime_directory()
        self.prefs=read_prefs()
        self.running=None
        self.running_id=None
        self.active_tab='drawing'
        self.buttons={}
        self.status=tk.StringVar(value='Choose a tool to open')
        self.path_labels={}
        self.create_gui()
        self.root.after(500,self.poll_process)
        self.root.after(120,lambda:self.root.lift())

    def mk_label(self,parent,txt,*,bold=False,size=9,color='text',bg='white',wrap=0):
        return tk.Label(parent,text=txt,bg=PALETTE[bg],fg=PALETTE[color],
                font=('Segoe UI',size,'bold' if bold else 'normal'),
                wraplength=wrap or 0,justify='left',anchor='w')

    def create_gui(self):
        top=tk.Frame(self.root,bg=PALETTE['pale'],height=60)
        top.pack(fill='x');top.pack_propagate(False)
        self.mk_label(top,'E3 Mega Tools',bold=True,size=15,bg='pale').pack(side='left',padx=16,pady=10)
        self.mk_label(top,'ALL-IN-ONE',size=9,color='blue',bg='pale',bold=True).pack(side='right',padx=17)
        nav=tk.Frame(self.root,bg=PALETTE['white'])
        nav.pack(fill='x',padx=14,pady=(11,5))
        self.nav_buttons={}
        for tab,label in (('drawing','Drawing Tools  (3)'),('excel','Excel Tools  (6)')):
            button=tk.Button(nav,text=label,font=('Segoe UI',10,'bold'),pady=7,
                bd=0,relief='flat',cursor='hand2',command=lambda t=tab:self.set_tab(t))
            button.pack(side='left',padx=(0,8))
            self.nav_buttons[tab]=button
        tk.Frame(self.root,bg=PALETTE['border'],height=1).pack(fill='x',padx=14)
        self.page=tk.Frame(self.root,bg=PALETTE['white'])
        self.page.pack(fill='both',expand=True,padx=14,pady=(12,6))
        self.footer=tk.Frame(self.root,bg=PALETTE['pale'])
        self.footer.pack(fill='x',padx=14,pady=(0,12))
        self.mk_label(self.footer,'',bg='pale',size=8).pack_forget()
        self.mk_label(self.footer,'Select a tool to open; only one runs at a time.',bg='pale',size=8,color='muted').pack(anchor='w',padx=11,pady=(7,0))
        self.mk_label(self.footer,'Test library/database changes on a saved E3 project copy.',bg='pale',size=8,color='muted').pack(anchor='w',padx=11,pady=(0,2))
        tk.Label(self.footer,textvariable=self.status,bg=PALETTE['pale'],
                 fg=PALETTE['text'],font=('Segoe UI',9,'bold'),anchor='w').pack(fill='x',padx=11,pady=(2,7))
        self.set_tab('drawing')

    def set_tab(self,tab):
        self.active_tab=tab
        for k,b in self.nav_buttons.items():
            selected=k==tab
            b.configure(bg=PALETTE['blue'] if selected else PALETTE['pale'],
                fg='#FFFFFF' if selected else PALETTE['text'],
                activebackground=PALETTE['blue_hover'] if selected else PALETTE['pale'],
                activeforeground='#FFFFFF' if selected else PALETTE['text'])
        for child in self.page.winfo_children():child.destroy()
        self.buttons={}
        if tab=='drawing':
            self.mk_label(self.page,'Formatting & layout',bold=True,size=12).pack(anchor='w',pady=(0,4))
            self.mk_label(self.page,'Uses the current E3 selection — no workbook required.',size=9,color='muted').pack(anchor='w',pady=(0,13))
        else:
            self.mk_label(self.page,'Workbook selection',bold=True,size=12).pack(anchor='w',pady=(0,2))
            self.mk_label(self.page,'Optional: choose one workbook for each tool group.',size=9,color='muted').pack(anchor='w',pady=(0,9))
            self.workbook_section()
            self.mk_label(self.page,'Spreadsheet tools',bold=True,size=12).pack(anchor='w',pady=(12,7))
        if tab=='excel':
            tool_grid=tk.Frame(self.page,bg=PALETTE['white'])
            tool_grid.pack(fill='x')
            tool_grid.grid_columnconfigure(0,weight=1,uniform='tool')
            tool_grid.grid_columnconfigure(1,weight=1,uniform='tool')
            excel_keys=[k for k,v in TOOLS.items() if v['tab']=='excel']
            for i,key in enumerate(excel_keys):
                self.add_tool_card(key,TOOLS[key],parent=tool_grid,grid=(i//2,i%2))
        else:
            for key,tool in TOOLS.items():
                if tool['tab']==tab:self.add_tool_card(key,tool)
        if tab=='drawing':
            self.mk_label(self.page,'The existing Header Formatter, Signal Mover and Pin Spacer are unchanged.',
                    size=9,color='muted',wrap=560).pack(anchor='w',pady=(10,0))
        actions=tk.Frame(self.page,bg=PALETTE['white'])
        actions.pack(fill='x',side='bottom',pady=(10,5))
        for name,command in [('Open logs / files',lambda:open_folder(self.runtime)),
                             ('Import pin data',self.import_pin_data)]:
            tk.Button(actions,text=name,command=command,fg=PALETTE['blue'],bg=PALETTE['white'],
                     font=('Segoe UI',8),bd=0,cursor='hand2').pack(side='left',padx=(0,12))
        tk.Button(actions,text='Close',command=self.close,fg=PALETTE['blue'],bg=PALETTE['white'],
                 font=('Segoe UI',8),bd=0,cursor='hand2').pack(side='right')
        self.disable_buttons(self.running is None)

    def workbook_section(self):
        for key,config in WORKBOOKS.items():
            line=tk.Frame(self.page,bg=PALETTE['panel'],height=36,
                highlightbackground=PALETTE['border'],highlightthickness=1)
            line.pack(fill='x',pady=(0,4));line.pack_propagate(False)
            left=tk.Frame(line,bg=PALETTE['panel'])
            left.pack(side='left',fill='both',expand=True,padx=(9,0),pady=4)
            self.mk_label(left,config['label'],size=9,bold=True,bg='panel').pack(side='left')
            text=tk.StringVar()
            self.path_labels[key]=text
            tk.Label(left,textvariable=text,bg=PALETTE['panel'],fg=PALETTE['muted'],
                font=('Segoe UI',8),anchor='w').pack(side='left',padx=9,fill='x',expand=True)
            for label,command in [('Choose',lambda k=key:self.choose_workbook(k)),
                                  ('Clear',lambda k=key:self.clear_workbook(k))]:
                tk.Button(line,text=label,command=command,bg=PALETTE['pale'],fg=PALETTE['blue'],
                    font=('Segoe UI',8),bd=0,padx=7,pady=3,cursor='hand2').pack(side='right',padx=(0,6))
            self.update_workbook_label(key)

    def update_workbook_label(self,key):
        if key in self.path_labels:
            value=self.prefs.get(key,'')
            if value:
                label=Path(value).name
                if len(label)>29:label=label[:26]+'...'
                self.path_labels[key].set(label)
            else:self.path_labels[key].set('Use tool picker')

    def choose_workbook(self,key):
        prior=self.prefs.get(key,'')
        result=filedialog.askopenfilename(parent=self.root,
            title='Choose '+WORKBOOKS[key]['label'],
            initialdir=str(Path(prior).parent) if prior else str(Path.home()),
            filetypes=[('Excel workbooks','*.xlsx *.xlsm'),('Excel files','*.xlsx'),('All files','*.*')])
        if not result:return
        self.prefs[key]=str(Path(result).resolve())
        write_prefs(self.prefs)
        self.update_workbook_label(key)
        self.status.set(WORKBOOKS[key]['label']+' selected')

    def clear_workbook(self,key):
        self.prefs[key]=''
        write_prefs(self.prefs)
        self.update_workbook_label(key)
        self.status.set(WORKBOOKS[key]['label']+' cleared; tool picker will open')

    def add_tool_card(self,key,tool,parent=None,grid=None):
        parent=parent or self.page
        compact=grid is not None
        box=tk.Frame(parent,bg=PALETTE['panel'],height=67 if compact else 57,
                     highlightbackground=PALETTE['border'],highlightthickness=1)
        if compact:
            row,col=grid
            box.grid(row=row,column=col,sticky='ew',pady=(0,6),padx=(0,6) if col==0 else (0,0))
            box.grid_propagate(False)
        else:
            box.pack(fill='x',pady=(0,5));box.pack_propagate(False)
        details=tk.Frame(box,bg=PALETTE['panel'])
        details.pack(side='left',fill='both',expand=True,padx=(9 if compact else 12,4),pady=(9,0))
        self.mk_label(details,tool['name'],bold=True,size=9 if compact else 10,bg='panel').pack(fill='x')
        short={
            'cable_maker':'Create a cable in DBE',
            'cable_placer':'Place cable and symbols',
            'block_maker':'Build and format blocks',
            'block_pin_arranger':'Position pins from Excel',
            'block_pin_formatter':'Format pin labels only',
            'block_signal_adder':'Import local signal names',
        }
        self.mk_label(details,short.get(key,tool['description']) if compact else tool['description'],
                      size=8,color='muted',bg='panel',wrap=175 if compact else 0).pack(fill='x')
        button=tk.Button(box,text='Open',font=('Segoe UI',9,'bold'),
                   bg=PALETTE['blue'],fg='white',width=6 if compact else 8,bd=0,
                   activebackground=PALETTE['blue_hover'],activeforeground='white',
                   relief='flat',pady=5,cursor='hand2',command=lambda k=key:self.launch(k))
        button.pack(side='right',padx=7 if compact else 11,pady=11)
        self.buttons[key]=button

    def disable_buttons(self,enabled):
        for button in self.buttons.values():
            button.configure(state='normal' if enabled else 'disabled',
                bg=PALETTE['blue'] if enabled else PALETTE['disabled'])

    def launch(self,key):
        if self.running is not None and self.running.poll() is None:
            messagebox.showinfo('One tool at a time','Close the running E3 tool first.',parent=self.root)
            return
        tool=TOOLS[key]
        group=tool['workbook']
        workbook=self.prefs.get(group,'') if group else ''
        if workbook and not Path(workbook).is_file():
            messagebox.showerror('Workbook not found',
                'The saved workbook no longer exists:\n'+workbook+
                '\n\nChoose a new workbook or clear this selection.',parent=self.root)
            return
        try:
            script,notes=install_tool(key,self.runtime)
            env=os.environ.copy()
            # Clear stale inherited choices when a workbook is not selected.
            for group_name in WORKBOOKS:
                env.pop('E3_MEGA_'+group_name.upper()+'_WORKBOOK',None)
            if workbook:
                env['E3_MEGA_'+group.upper()+'_WORKBOOK']=workbook
            cmd=[sys.executable,str(script)]
            if key=='pin':cmd.append('--worker')
            flags=getattr(subprocess,'CREATE_NO_WINDOW',0) if sys.platform=='win32' else 0
            logfile=script.parent/'launcher_output.log'
            with logfile.open('a',encoding='utf-8') as log:
                log.write('\n===== '+datetime.now().isoformat(timespec='seconds')+' '+key+
                         ' ('+SUITE_VERSION+') =====\n')
                for note in notes:log.write('install: '+note+'\n')
                if workbook:log.write('workbook: '+workbook+'\n')
            with logfile.open('a',encoding='utf-8') as log:
                self.running=subprocess.Popen(cmd,cwd=str(script.parent),env=env,
                    stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                    creationflags=flags,close_fds=True)
            self.running_id=key
            self.disable_buttons(False)
            self.status.set(tool['name']+' is running')
            self.root.attributes('-topmost',False)
        except Exception as exc:
            self.running=None;self.running_id=None
            self.disable_buttons(True)
            self.status.set('Failed to launch '+tool['name'])
            messagebox.showerror('E3 Tools launcher error',str(exc),parent=self.root)

    def poll_process(self):
        try:
            if self.running is not None:
                returncode=self.running.poll()
                if returncode is not None:
                    name=TOOLS[self.running_id]['name']
                    self.running=None;self.running_id=None
                    self.disable_buttons(True)
                    self.status.set(name+(' completed / closed' if returncode==0 else ' failed — see logs'))
                    self.root.attributes('-topmost',True)
        finally:
            self.root.after(500,self.poll_process)

    def import_pin_data(self):
        directory=filedialog.askdirectory(parent=self.root,title='Locate previous pin spacer recovery files')
        if not directory:return
        destination=self.runtime/'cable_pin_spacer'
        destination.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        imported=[]
        for suffix in ('_recovery.json','_pin_identity.json'):
            for name in ('main'+suffix,'E3_Cable_To_Block_Pin_Spacing_V15_ZUKEN_LIGHT_GUI'+suffix):
                original=Path(directory)/name
                dest=destination/('main'+suffix)
                if original.is_file() and not dest.exists():
                    data=json.loads(original.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
                    if not isinstance(data,dict):raise ValueError('Expected JSON object in '+name)
                    shutil.copy2(original,dest)
                    imported.append(dest.name)
                    break
        messagebox.showinfo('Pin Spacer recovery data',
            'Imported: '+(', '.join(imported) or 'none')+'\nExisting files were left untouched.',
            parent=self.root)

    def close(self):
        if self.running is not None and self.running.poll() is None:
            if not messagebox.askyesno('Close launcher?',
                'A tool is still running. Close only the launcher and let it finish?',parent=self.root):
                return
        self.root.destroy()


def main():
    if '--self-test' in sys.argv or '--diagnose' in sys.argv:
        assert len(TOOLS)==9 and len(SOURCE_FILES)==13
        for filename,code in SOURCE_FILES.items():
            compile(code,filename,'exec')
            print('OK:',filename,len(code.splitlines()),'lines',source_digest(code)[:14])
        print('PASS: 9 tool entries / 13 readable embedded Python files. No E3/SQL invoked.')
        return
    if '--list-tools' in sys.argv:
        for key,info in TOOLS.items():print(key+': '+info['entry'])
        return
    if '--extract' in sys.argv:
        output=runtime_directory()
        for key in TOOLS:
            entry,notes=install_tool(key,output)
            print(key+': '+str(entry))
            for note in notes:print('  '+note)
        return
    MegaApp().root.mainloop()

if __name__=='__main__':
    main()
